\displaystyle\qquad-w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})))\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}))\bigg]^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\mathbf{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\bigg[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i})\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}\left(h_{i-1}\left(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})\right)\right)-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}\left(H_{i-1}\left(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})\right)\right)\right)\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}))\bigg]^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\mathbf{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\bigg[\left|w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i})\right|\left|h_{i-1}\left(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})\right)-H_{i-1}\left(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})\right)\right|\left|\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}))\right|\bigg]^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\mathbf{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\bigg[\left|w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i})\right|\left(\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}))\right)^{2}\bigg]^{2}\bigg]/N

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}/N,

by Lemmas
11
and
13
. Thus,
it remains to show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}{\bf E}\bigg[\bigg(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})))\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}))\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1})\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]=O_{T}(1/N).

This is shown by applying Lemma
12
,
where we take
\mathbf{i}=i-1
,
\mathbf{i}^{\prime}=i
,
S^{\prime}=\left\{C_{k}\left(j_{k}\right):\;j_{k}\in\left[N_{k}\right],\;k<i-1\right\}

and

f\left(S,c_{i-1},c_{i}\right)=w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,c_{i-1}).

Observe that
f\left(S,c_{i-1},c_{i}\right)
is independent of
C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right)
.
In addition:

\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\left(f\left(S^{j_{i-1}},C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\leq K\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\left(w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i})\right)^{4}\right]^{1/2}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}))\right)^{4}\right]^{1/2}\leq K_{T}

from Lemma
13
. This shows the claim.
∎

Lemma 16
.

Under Assumption
1
,
we have for any
t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{L}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{L},x)

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{L}^{\tilde{H}}\left(t,x,c_{L}\right)\varphi_{L}^{\prime\prime}(H_{L}(t,x,c_{L}))+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/4}),

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i-1},x)+\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i},x)\right]+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/4}).

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i-1}(c_{i-1})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i-1}(c_{i-1})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i-1},x)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\right)\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\right)\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right].

We proceed with analyzing each term.

First estimate.

Firstly we claim that

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\bigg[\bigg(\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=O_{T}(1/N).

Indeed, this is obvious for
i=L
, and for
i<L
, we verify the
condition of Lemma
12
for
{\bf i}=i
,

{\bf i}^{\prime}=i-1
,
S^{\prime}=\left\{C_{k}\left(j_{k}\right):\;j_{k}\in\left[N_{k}\right],\;k<i\right\}

and

f(S,c_{i},c_{i-1})=\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\right)w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}.

Observe that
f
is independent of
C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)
. In
this case, we only need to bound the following:

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1})-H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)^{4}\right]+K{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left(w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T},

by Lemmas
11
and
13
. This
proves the claim.

Next, we extend this claim. In particular,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\bigg[\bigg(\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}\left(t,x,C_{i-1}\right)\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{N}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\bigg[\bigg(\left(\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}\left(t,x,C_{i-1}\right)\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K_{T}}{N},

by Lemmas
11
and
13
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\bigg[\bigg(\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}\left(t,x,C_{i-1}\right)\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=O_{T}(1/N).

Second estimate.

We have:

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\bigg[\bigg(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i},x)\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=O(N^{-1/2}).

This is again obvious for
i=L
. For
i<L
, by applying Lemma
12

for
{\bf i}=i
,
{\bf i}^{\prime}=i-1
and

f(S,c_{i},c_{i-1})=\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i},x),

we have the claim since firstly it is easy to see that by Lemma
11
,

\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\left(f\left(S,C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right),C_{i-1}\right)-f\left(S^{j_{i}},C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right),C_{i-1}\right)\right)^{2}\right]=O_{T}\left(1/N\right),

and secondly

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i},x)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left(w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right)^{4}\right]+K{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\left(\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i},x)\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}.

by Lemmas
11
and
14
.

Last estimate.

Finally we have:

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\left(\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right]^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\left(\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1})\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right]^{2}\right]/N

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}/N.

by Lemmas
11
,
13
and
14
.

Combining estimates.

Combining all above estimates, we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i-1},x)+\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i},x)\right]+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/4}).

Note that the claim for
\tilde{G}_{L}^{\partial\tilde{H}}
follows
by the same argument.
∎

Lemma 17
.

Under Assumption
1
, we have
for any
t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{1}^{w}(t,1,c_{1},x)

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{1}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{1},x)x+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i},x)\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))+\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\tilde{G}^{y}\left(t,x\right)

\displaystyle=\varphi_{L}^{\prime}\left(H_{L}\left(t,1,x\right)\right)\tilde{G}_{L}^{\tilde{H}}\left(t,1,x\right)+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}).

Proof.

We prove the second statement; the claims for
\tilde{G}_{1}^{w}

and
\tilde{G}^{y}
can be proven similarly. We have:

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(c_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}\right)\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))+\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(c_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}\right)\left(\varphi_{i-1}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\right)

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i},x)\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))+\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}).

Here, we have used that by Lemmas
11
and
13
,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\bigg[\bigg(\sqrt{N}\left(\varphi_{i-1}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}

\displaystyle\qquad-\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i-1},x)\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{N}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\bigg[\left(\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i-1},x)\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K_{T}}{N},

and that by Lemmas
13
and
14
,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\bigg[\bigg(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(C_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right)\left(\varphi_{i-1}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))-\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right)\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{N}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left(\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i-1},x)\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K_{T}}{N}.

∎

B.3
Structure of the limiting Gaussian process and the proof of Theorem

2

Each process
\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial H},\;\tilde{G}_{i}^{H},\;\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}},\;\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}},\;\tilde{G}_{i}^{w},\;\tilde{G}^{y}

on
t\leq T
can be viewed as an element of
{\cal G}_{T}=C([0,T],L^{2}(\mathbb{X}\times\tilde{P}))

where
\tilde{P}
is
P_{i}
or
P_{i-1}\times P_{i}
. Equip
{\cal G}_{T}

with the norm

\|g\|_{T}=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|g\left(t\right)\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}.

We define the following Gaussian processes
\underline{G}^{\partial H},\;\underline{G}^{H}

with zero mean and covariance given by

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(t,c_{i},x)\underline{G}_{j}^{H}(t^{\prime},c_{j}^{\prime},x^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\delta_{ij}\mathbb{C}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1},x));\;w_{j}(t^{\prime},C_{i-1},c^{\prime}_{j})\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t^{\prime},C_{i-1},x^{\prime}))\right],&i>1,\\
0,&{\rm otherwise,}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\underline{G}_{i}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i},x)\underline{G}_{j}^{\partial H}(t^{\prime},c_{j}^{\prime},x^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\delta_{ij}\mathbb{C}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\varphi_{i}^{\prime}(H_{i}(t,x,c_{i}))w_{i}(t,c_{i},C_{i+1}){\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i+1}(C_{i+1})}};\;\varphi_{i}^{\prime}(H_{i}(t^{\prime},x^{\prime},c_{j}^{\prime}))w_{i}(t^{\prime},c_{j}^{\prime},C_{i+1}){\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t^{\prime},x^{\prime})}{\partial H_{i+1}(C_{i+1})}}\right],&i<L,\\
0,&{\rm otherwise,}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(t,c_{i},x)\underline{G}_{j}^{\partial H}(t^{\prime},c_{j}^{\prime},x^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\delta_{i,j+2}\mathbb{C}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\varphi_{i-2}^{\prime}(H_{i-2}(t,x,c_{j}^{\prime}))w_{i-1}(t,c_{j}^{\prime},C_{i-1}){\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i-1}(C_{i-1})}};\;w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\right],&i>2,\\
0,&{\rm otherwise.}\end{cases}

As a side remark, note that the covariance of
\underline{G}^{\partial H}

and
\underline{G}^{H}
can be recognized through the following simple
rule: for functions
f(C_{i})
and
g(C_{j})
, we have

{\bf E}\left[\sqrt{N}(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}[f(C_{i}(J_{i}))]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}[f(C_{i})])\cdot\sqrt{N}(\mathbb{E}_{J_{j}}[g(C_{j}(J_{j}))]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{j}}[g(C_{j})])\right]=\delta_{ij}(\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}[f(C_{i})g(C_{i})]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}[f(C_{i})]\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}[g(C_{i})]).

(7)

Define
\underline{G}^{\tilde{H}},\underline{G}^{\partial\tilde{H}},\underline{G}^{w},\underline{G}^{y}

by the following linear combination of
\underline{G}^{H}
and
\underline{G}^{\partial H}
:

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{1},x)

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(t,c_{i},x)+\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\underline{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,C_{i-1})\right],

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{L}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{L},x)

\displaystyle=\varphi_{L}^{\prime\prime}(H_{L}(t,x,c_{L}))\underline{G}_{L}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,c_{L}),

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{i-1}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)

\displaystyle=\underline{G}_{i-1}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i-1},x)+\underline{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x)\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\underline{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right],

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{1}^{w}(t,1,c_{1},x)

\displaystyle=\underline{G}_{1}^{\partial\tilde{H}}\left(t,c_{1},x\right)x,

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\underline{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i},x)\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))+\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}\underline{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(t,c_{i-1},x),

\displaystyle\underline{G}^{y}(t,x)

\displaystyle=\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(H_{L}(t,x,1))\underline{G}_{L}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x,1).

Recall that we say
\tilde{G}
converges
G
-polynomially in moment
to a Gaussian process
\underline{G}
if for any square-integrable

f_{j}:\;\mathbb{T}\times\Omega_{i(j)-1}\times\Omega_{i(j)}\times\mathbb{X}\to\mathbb{R}

which is continuous in time,

\sup_{t_{j}\leq T}\bigg|\mathbf{E}\bigg[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\bigg]-\mathbf{E}\bigg[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\underline{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\bigg]\bigg|=O_{D,T}\big(\sup_{t\leq T}\max_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t}^{D}\big)\cdot N^{-1/8},

where
D=\sum_{j}\alpha_{j}\beta_{j}
. (For
\tilde{G}^{H}
,
\tilde{G}^{\tilde{H}}
,

\tilde{G}^{\partial H}
and
\tilde{G}^{\partial\tilde{H}}
, we
take
f_{j}:\mathbb{T}\times\Omega_{i(j)}\times\mathbb{X}\to\mathbb{R}
.)
We restate and prove Theorem
2
.

Theorem 18

(Theorem
2
restated)
.

We have
\tilde{G}
converges
G
-polynomially
in moment to
\underline{G}
, where
\underline{G}
is the Gaussian
process with mean and covariance structure as given above.

Proof.

First, we show the following:

\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{\ell}\left\langle f_{\ell},\left(\tilde{G}_{i(\ell)}^{\tau(\ell)}\right)^{\alpha_{\ell}}\right\rangle_{t_{\ell}}^{\beta_{\ell}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{\ell}\left\langle f_{\ell},\left(\underline{G}_{i(\ell)}^{\tau(\ell)}\right)^{\alpha_{\ell}}\right\rangle_{t_{\ell}}^{\beta_{\ell}}\right]\right|=O_{D}(\max_{\ell}\|f_{\ell}\|_{t_{\ell}}^{D})\cdot N^{-1/2},

where for each
\ell
,
\tau(\ell)
is either
H
or
\partial H
.
By independence of
C_{i}(j_{i})
for distinct
i
’s, it suffices
to consider the case where
i(\ell)=i
for all
\ell
.

Consider the case
\tau(\ell)=H
and
i\left(\ell\right)=i
, we
have

\displaystyle\left\langle f_{\ell},\left(\tilde{G}_{i}^{H}\right)^{\alpha_{\ell}}\right\rangle_{t_{\ell}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\left[f_{\ell}(t_{\ell},C_{i},X)\left(\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[Z_{i}\left(t_{\ell},J_{i-1},C_{i},X\right)\right]\right)^{\alpha_{\ell}}\right],

where we denote

Z_{i}(t_{\ell},j_{i-1},c_{i},x)=w_{i}(t_{\ell},C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t_{\ell},x,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})))-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}[w_{i}(t_{\ell},C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t_{\ell},x,C_{i-1}))].

One can write
\prod_{\ell}\left\langle f_{\ell},\left(\tilde{G}_{i}^{H}\right)^{\alpha_{\ell}}\right\rangle_{t_{\ell}}^{\beta_{\ell}}

as a sum of terms of the form:

N^{-D/2}\prod_{\ell}\prod_{r\leq\beta_{\ell}}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[f_{\ell}(t_{\ell},C_{i},X)\prod_{h\leq\alpha_{\ell}}Z_{i}(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X)\bigg].

Note that
{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[Z_{i}(\cdot,J_{i-1},C_{i},\cdot)\right]=0
.
Thus, if there is any index
j_{i-1}
for which
j_{i-1}
appears
exactly once among
j_{\ell,r,h}
, then the above term after taking

\mathbf{E}
vanishes. The number of terms with each
j_{i-1}
appearing
either zero or exactly twice among
j_{\ell,r,h}
is
\Theta(N^{D/2})
.
Notice that these terms make up the quantity
\mathbf{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell}\Big<f_{\ell},\left(\underline{G}_{i(\ell)}^{\tau(\ell)}\right)^{\alpha_{\ell}}\Big>_{t_{\ell}}^{\beta_{\ell}}\bigg]
,
which is to be subtracted away. The number of remaining terms where
no
j_{i-1}
appears exactly once is at most
O(N^{(D-1)/2})
. Thus
we are done upon bounding

{\bf E}\bigg[\bigg|\prod_{\ell}\prod_{r\leq\beta_{\ell}}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[f_{\ell}(t_{\ell},C_{i},X)\prod_{h\leq\alpha_{\ell}}Z_{i}(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X)\bigg]\bigg|\bigg].

By Lemma
11
, it is easy to see that

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{X,C_{i}}\left[|Z_{i}(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X)|^{D}\right]\leq K_{T,D}.

Then:

\displaystyle{\bf E}\bigg[\bigg|\prod_{\ell}\prod_{r\leq\beta_{\ell}}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[f_{\ell}\left(t_{\ell},C_{i},X\right)\prod_{h\leq\alpha_{\ell}}Z_{i}\left(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X\right)\bigg]\bigg|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{\ell}\prod_{r\leq\beta_{\ell}}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[\Big|f_{\ell}\left(t_{\ell},C_{i},X\right)\Big|^{2}\bigg]^{1/2}{\bf E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[\prod_{h\leq\alpha_{\ell}}\bigg|Z_{i}\left(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X\right)\bigg|^{2}\bigg]^{1/2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\ell}\|f_{\ell}\|_{t_{\ell}}^{D}\prod_{\ell}\prod_{r\leq\beta_{\ell}}{\bf E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[\prod_{h\leq\alpha_{\ell}}\bigg|Z_{i}\left(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X\right)\bigg|^{2}\bigg]^{1/2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\ell}\|f_{\ell}\|_{t_{\ell}}^{D}\prod_{\ell}\prod_{r\leq\beta_{\ell}}{\bf E}\bigg[\prod_{h\leq\alpha_{\ell}}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[\bigg|Z_{i}\left(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X\right)\bigg|^{2\alpha_{\ell}}\bigg]^{1/\left(2\alpha_{\ell}\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\ell}\|f_{\ell}\|_{t_{\ell}}^{D}\prod_{\ell}\prod_{r\leq\beta_{\ell}}\prod_{h\leq\alpha_{\ell}}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i}}\bigg[\bigg|Z_{i}\left(t_{\ell},j_{\ell,r,h},C_{i},X\right)\bigg|^{2\alpha_{\ell}}\bigg]^{1/\left(2\alpha_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\ell}\|f_{\ell}\|_{t_{\ell}}^{D}\cdot K_{T,D}.

This completes the proof for the case
\tau(\ell)=H
and
i\left(\ell\right)=i
.

The involvement of terms with
\tau(\ell)=\partial H
can be dealt
with similarly. To deal with the case
\tau\left(\ell\right)\in\left\{H,\partial H\right\}
,
we note that the same argument holds with an appropriate modification
of the definition of
Z_{i}
; in particular, this function now depends
on
\ell
via
\tau\left(\ell\right)
, but this detail does not
affect the argument since all we need is that
{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[Z_{i}(\cdot,J_{i-1},C_{i},\cdot)\right]=0

and
{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{X,C_{i}}\left[|Z_{i}(t,\cdot,C_{i},X)|^{D}\right]\leq K_{T,D}.

This completes the argument that
(\tilde{G}^{\partial H},\tilde{G}^{H})\to(\underline{G}^{\partial H},\underline{G}^{H})

in
G
-polynomial moment.

Finally, observe that
\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}},\;\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}},\;\tilde{G}_{i}^{w},\;\tilde{G}^{y}

can be written as a linear combination of
\tilde{G}_{i}^{H}
and

\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial H}
by Lemmas
15
,

16
and
17
, from which
it is easy to verify that
\tilde{G}\to\underline{G}
in
G
-polynomial
moment.
∎

B.4
Dynamical form of the limiting Gaussian process

We also have the following property of the limiting process
\underline{G}

that will be useful later. Define further auxiliary processes:

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{i-1}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)}(t,c_{i-1},x)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]\right)\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))\partial_{t}H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}),

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right]\right)\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1}))

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(J_{i}))\partial_{t}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i})\partial_{t}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i})}\right)\right]\right)\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,c_{i-1})),

and

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{i}^{\partial_{t}H}(t,c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}[\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})))]-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}[\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))]\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{N}\Big(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})))\partial_{t}H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}))]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1}))\partial_{t}H_{i-1}(t,x,C_{i-1})]\Big),

with
\tilde{G}_{L-1}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)}=\tilde{G}_{L}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)}=\tilde{G}_{1}^{\partial_{t}H}=0
.
As above, we can show that jointly with previously defined processes

\tilde{G}^{H},\tilde{G}^{\partial H}
,
\tilde{G}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)},\tilde{G}^{\partial_{t}H}

converges to Gaussian processes
\underline{G}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)},\underline{G}^{\partial_{t}H}

whose covariance structure (jointly with the previously defined processes

\underline{G}
) can be deduced by following the rule (
7
).

Theorem 19
.

The limiting Gaussian process
(\underline{G}^{H},\underline{G}^{\partial H})

is the solution to the ODE

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(t,c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\underline{G}_{i}^{\partial_{t}H}(t,c_{i},x),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\underline{G}_{i}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\underline{G}_{i}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)}(t,c_{i},x).

Similarly we can express
\partial_{t}\underline{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}

and
\partial_{t}\underline{G}_{i}^{\partial\tilde{H}}
in terms
of
\partial_{t}G_{i}^{H},\partial_{t}G_{i}^{\partial H}
using the
expression of
G^{\tilde{H}}
and
G^{\partial\tilde{H}}
in terms
of
G^{H}
and
G^{\partial H}
. Finally, we have

\partial_{t}\underline{G}^{y}(t,x)=\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(H_{L}(t,1,x))\partial_{t}\underline{G}_{L}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x)+\partial_{t}H_{L}(t,1,x)\varphi_{L}^{\prime\prime}(H_{L}(t,1,x))\underline{G}_{L}^{\tilde{H}}(t,x).

Proof.

Defining
\overline{G}_{i}^{H}(t,c_{i},x),\overline{G}_{i}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i},x)

as solutions to

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\overline{G}_{i}^{H}(t,c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=G_{i}^{\partial_{t}H}(t,c_{i},x),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\overline{G}_{i}^{\partial H}(t,c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=G_{i}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)}(t,c_{i},x),

it is straightforward to verify that
\overline{G}
has the same
covariance structure as
G
. Since
G^{\tilde{H}},G^{\partial\tilde{H}},G^{w},G^{y}

are linear combinations of
G^{H},G^{\partial H}
, the conclusions
for
\partial_{t}G
easily follow.
∎

Remark 20
.

We can in fact show that jointly with
\tilde{G}^{H}
and
\tilde{G}^{\partial H}
,
we have
(\partial_{t}\tilde{G}^{H},\partial_{t}\tilde{G}^{\partial H})

converges in
G
-polynomial moment to
(\partial_{t}G^{H},\partial_{t}G^{\partial H})
,
and this extends by linearity to
\partial_{t}\tilde{G}^{\tilde{H}},\partial_{t}\tilde{G}^{\partial\tilde{H}},\partial_{t}\tilde{G}^{w},\partial_{t}\tilde{G}^{y}
,
by following the procedure leading to Theorem
18
. Since
we will not need to use this fact, we omit the details of the proof.

Appendix C
Existence and uniqueness of
R
: Proof of Theorem
3

For
G\in{\cal G}
and
p\geq 1
, define

\|G\|_{T,2p}^{2p}=\sup_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C}\left[\left|G^{y}(t,X)\right|^{2p}+\sum_{i=1}^{L}\left|G_{i}^{w}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i},X)\right|^{2p}\right].

Theorem 21
.

Under Assumption
1
, for
any
\epsilon>0
and all
G\in{\cal G}
with
\|G\|_{T,2+\epsilon}<\infty
,
there exists a unique solution
R_{i}(G,\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)\in L^{2}(P_{i-1}\times P_{i})

which is continuous in time. Furthermore, for each
t\leq T
,
R(G,t)

is a continuous linear functional in
G
and
\|R\left(G\right)\|_{T,2}\leq K_{T,\epsilon}\|G\|_{T,2+\epsilon}
.
In fact, for each
B
sufficiently large in
T
, there exists a
sequence in
B
of processes
R^{B}
which is a continuous linear
functional in
G
with

\|R^{B}(G)\|_{T,2}^{2}\leq\exp(K_{T}B)\|G\|_{T,2}^{2}

for all
G
with
\|G\|_{T,2}<\infty
, and for
G
with
\|G\|_{T,2+\epsilon}<\infty
,

\|R(G)-R^{B}(G)\|_{T,2}^{2}\leq\|G\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2}\exp(-K_{T}\epsilon B^{2}/\left(2+\epsilon\right)).

Here we define

\left\|R\left(G\right)\right\|_{T,2}^{2}=\sup_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{L}\left|R_{i}\left(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\right],

and similar for
\|R(G)-R^{B}(G)\|_{T,2}^{2}
.

The main technical difficulty in the following proof lies in the fact
that the weights
w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})
could be unbounded, in
which case the linear operator
R\mapsto\partial_{t}R
is unbounded.
Our proof of both existence and uniqueness follows from a delicate
truncation scheme. This scheme requires careful treatment: there is
no a priori bound on
R
again due to the unboundedness problem,
and as such, usual truncation argument does not work. We remark that
this problem is unique to the multilayer structure; we do not encounter
the same problem for shallow networks.

Proof of Theorem
21
.

Let us define:

\displaystyle\Delta F_{i}^{\left(1\right)}(R)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{Z}\bigg[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{r}\left(G,t,C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial w_{r}\left(C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)}\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\bigg[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)\right)\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[\sum_{r=1}^{L}R_{r}\left(G,t,C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial w_{r}\left(C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(c_{i-1},c_{i}\right)}\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\bigg[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)\right)\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i-1}\left(G,t,C_{i-2},c_{i-1}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}\left(C_{i-2},c_{i-1}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(c_{i-1},c_{i}\right)}\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\bigg[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)\right)\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i}\left(G,t,C_{i-1},c_{i}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}\left(C_{i-1},c_{i}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(c_{i-1},c_{i}\right)}\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\bigg[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)\right)\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i+1}\left(G,t,c_{i},C_{i+1}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}\left(c_{i},C_{i+1}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(c_{i-1},c_{i}\right)}\bigg]\bigg],

\displaystyle\Delta F_{i}^{\left(2\right)}(R)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{Z}\bigg[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)\right)G^{y}\left(t,X\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\bigg[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)\right)G_{i}^{w}\left(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X\right)\bigg],

\displaystyle\Delta F_{i}(R)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=\Delta F_{i}^{\left(1\right)}(R)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})+\Delta F_{i}^{\left(2\right)}(R)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i}).

In this notation,
\partial_{t}R_{i}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=-\Delta F_{i}(R)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})
.
Let

\displaystyle|R|_{t,2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}[\left|R_{j}(G,t,C_{j-1},C_{j})\right|^{2p}],

\displaystyle|R_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t,2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}[\left|R_{i+1}(G,t,c_{i},C_{i+1})\right|^{2p}],

\displaystyle|R_{i}(c_{i})|_{t,2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}[\left|R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\right|{}^{2p}].

We define
|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t,2p},\;|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t,2p},\;|G_{i}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t,2p},\;|G_{i}^{w}(c_{i-1})|_{t,2p},\;|G_{i}^{y}|_{t,2p}

similarly (where the last three should include
\mathbb{E}_{Z}
in
addition). We define

\left|G_{i}^{w}\right|_{t,2p}^{2p}=\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|G_{i}^{w}\left(C_{i}\right)\right|_{t,2p}^{2p}+\left|G_{i}^{w}\left(C_{i-1}\right)\right|_{t,2p}^{2p}\right],\quad|G|_{t,2p}^{2p}=\left|G^{y}\right|_{t,2p}^{2p}+\sum_{i=1}^{L}\left|G_{i}^{w}\right|_{t,2p}^{2p}.

When we drop the subscript
2p
, we implicitly assume that
2p=2
.

Step 1: truncated process
R^{B}
.

For each threshold
B>1
, let

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}(\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})||_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i-1})|_{t},|w_{i-1}(c_{i-1})|_{t})\leq B)

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}(\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t})\leq B).

We define
R^{B}
as the solution to

\partial_{t}R_{i}^{B}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})]=-\Delta F_{i}(R^{B})(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\cdot\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i}).

We can rewrite – with an abuse of notations – in the following
form:

\partial_{t}R^{B}(G,t,\cdot,\cdot)=\mathfrak{A}_{t}^{B}(R^{B}(G,t))+H(G,t),

for
\mathfrak{A}_{t}^{B}
a linear operator. (One may easily recognize
that the first term corresponds to
\Delta F_{i}^{\left(1\right)}

and the second one corresponds to
\Delta F_{i}^{\left(2\right)}
.)

By Lemma
11
, we have the bound:

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{A}_{t}^{B}(R^{B}(G,t))(c_{i-1},c_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}|R^{B}|_{t}|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}+K_{T}|R^{B}|_{t}|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+K_{T}|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}+K_{T}|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+K_{T}|R_{i-1}^{B}(c_{i-1})|_{t}|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}.

Note that by Lemma
11
,

\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[|w_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}\right]\leq K_{T}.

Thus,

\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\mathfrak{A}_{t}^{B}(R^{B}(G,t))(C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\right]\leq K_{T}B^{2}|R^{B}|_{t}^{2}.

Existence and uniqueness of
R^{B}
follows immediately from boundedness
of
\mathfrak{A}^{B}
. Also
R^{B}
is a bounded linear functional in

G
.

Step 2: bounds for
R^{B}
.

We obtain a bound on
|R^{B}|_{t}
. Observe that

\left|H\left(G,t\right)\left(c_{i-1},c_{i}\right)\right|\leq K_{T}|w_{i+1}\left(c_{i}\right)|_{t}\left|G^{y}\right|_{t}+K\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|G_{i}^{w}\left(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X\right)\right|\right]

and as such,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(|R_{i}^{B}|_{t}^{2})

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|R_{i}^{B}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2|R_{i}^{B}|_{t}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\partial R_{i}^{B}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}|R^{B}|_{t}^{2}+K_{T}B|R_{i}^{B}|_{t}^{2}+K_{T}|G|_{t}^{2}.

This implies

\partial_{t}(|R^{B}|_{t}^{2})\leq K_{T}B|R^{B}|_{t}^{2}+K_{T}|G|_{t}^{2},

so by Gronwall’s lemma, for any
t\leq T
,

|R^{B}|_{t}\leq\exp(K_{T}B)\sup_{s\leq T}|G|_{s}=\exp(K_{T}B)\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}.

Now let us zoom into
R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})
and
R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})
.
From the bounds on
\left|\mathfrak{A}_{t}^{B}(R^{B}(G,t))(c_{i},c_{i+1})\right|

and
\left|H\left(G,t\right)\left(c_{i},c_{i+1}\right)\right|
, we
deduce:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}^{2})

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[R_{i+1}^{B}(G,t,c_{i},C_{i+1})\partial_{t}R_{i+1}^{B}(G,t,c_{i},C_{i+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}\left(|R^{B}|_{t}+|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+\left|G^{y}\right|_{t}+|G_{i+1}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t}\right),

which yields

\partial_{t}|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}\leq K_{T}\left(\exp(K_{T}B)\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}+|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+\left|G^{y}\right|_{t}+|G_{i+1}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t}\right).

Similarly:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}^{2})

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[R_{i}^{B}(G,t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\partial_{t}R_{i}^{B}(G,t,C_{i-1},c_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}\Big((1+|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t})(1+|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t})|R^{B}|_{t}+|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|G_{i}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t}+|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}|G^{y}|_{t}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}\left(B^{2}|R^{B}|_{t}+B|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|G_{i}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t}+|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}|G^{y}|_{t}\right),

which yields

\displaystyle\partial_{t}|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}\left(\exp(K_{T}B)\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}+B|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|G_{i}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t}+B|G^{y}|_{t}\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}\left(\exp(K_{T}B)\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}+B\left(|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}\right)+|G_{i}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t}+|G_{i+1}^{w}(c_{i})|_{t}+B|G^{y}|_{t}\right).

By Gronwall’s lemma,

|R_{i}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}+|R_{i+1}^{B}(c_{i})|_{t}\leq\left(\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left(|G_{i}^{w}(c_{i})|_{s}+|G_{i+1}^{w}(c_{i})|_{s}+|G^{y}|_{s}\right)ds\right)\exp\left(K_{T}B\right).

Step 3: taking
B\to\infty
.

Next we compare
R^{B}
and
R^{B^{\prime}}
for
B^{\prime}>B
. Let us define

\Delta R_{i}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=R_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})-R_{i}^{B}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i}).

We have that if
\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=1
, then

\partial_{t}\Delta R_{i}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=-\Delta F_{i}^{\left(1\right)}(\Delta R)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i}).

If
\mathbb{B}^{B^{\prime}}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=1
but
\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=0
,
we have

\partial_{t}\Delta R_{i}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=-\Delta F_{i}(R^{B^{\prime}})(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i}).

Finally, if
\mathbb{B}^{B^{\prime}}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=0
, then

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\Delta R_{i}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=0.

With
\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=\mathbb{I}(\mathbb{B}^{B^{\prime}}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=1,\;\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=0)
,
we obtain:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\cdot\partial_{t}\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\partial_{t}\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]^{1/2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\Big(|R^{B^{\prime}}|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}+|R^{B^{\prime}}|_{t}^{2}|w_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+|R_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}+|R_{i+1}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}+|R_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i-1})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}+|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|G^{y}|_{t}^{2}+|G_{i}^{w}(C_{i-1},C_{i}|_{t}^{2}\Big)\bigg]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]^{1/2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\bigg((B^{\prime 2}+B^{\prime 4})|R^{B^{\prime}}|_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+B^{\prime 2}|R_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}+|R_{i+1}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}+B^{\prime 2}|R_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i-1})|_{t}^{2}+B^{\prime 2}|G^{y}|_{t}^{2}+|G_{i}^{w}(C_{i-1},C_{i}|_{t}^{2}\bigg)\bigg]^{1/2}.

Using Lemma
11
and the bounds in Step 2, we get:

\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\left(|R^{B^{\prime}}|_{t}^{2}(B^{\prime 2}+B^{\prime 4})\right)\right]\leq\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}^{2}\exp\left(K_{T}B^{\prime}-K_{T}B^{2}\right).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\left(B^{\prime 2}|R_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}+\|R_{i+1}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i})\|^{2}+B^{\prime 2}|R_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(C_{i-1})|_{t}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(K_{T}B^{\prime}\right)\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\left(\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left(|G_{i}^{w}(C_{i})|_{s}^{2}+|G_{i+1}^{w}(C_{i})|_{s}^{2}+|G_{i-1}^{w}(C_{i-1})|_{s}^{2}+|G_{i}^{w}(C_{i-1})|_{s}^{2}+|G^{y}|_{s}^{2}\right)ds\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(K_{T}B^{\prime}-K_{T}\frac{\epsilon B^{2}}{2+\epsilon}\right)\left\|G\right\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\left(B^{\prime 2}|G^{y}|_{t}^{2}+|G_{i}^{w}(C_{i-1},C_{i}|_{t}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(K_{T}B^{\prime}-K_{T}\frac{\epsilon B^{2}}{2+\epsilon}\right)\left\|G\right\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2}.

We thus get:

\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]\leq\left\|G\right\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2}\exp\left(K_{T}B^{\prime}-K_{T}\frac{\epsilon B^{2}}{2+\epsilon}\right).

The term where
\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=1
can be bounded
as in the bound for
R^{B}
:

\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\Delta R_{i}(G,t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right]\leq K_{T}B|\Delta R|_{t}^{2}.

The last two displays give:

\partial_{t}|\Delta R|_{t}^{2}\leq K_{T}B|\Delta R|_{t}^{2}+\left\|G\right\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2}\exp\left(K_{T}B^{\prime}-K_{T}\frac{\epsilon B^{2}}{2+\epsilon}\right).

Hence,

|\Delta R|_{t}^{2}\leq\left\|G\right\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2}\exp\left(K_{T}BB^{\prime}-K_{T}\frac{\epsilon B^{3}}{2+\epsilon}\right).

In particular, for all
B
sufficiently large, we have
|\Delta R|_{t}^{2}\leq\left\|G\right\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2}\exp(-K_{T}\epsilon B^{2}/(2+\epsilon))

for all
B^{\prime}\leq 2B
. Thus, we can easily deduce that
R^{B}
converges
in
L^{2}
to a limit, which is the process
R
, as
B\to\infty
.
Since
|\Delta R|_{t}^{2}
decays exponentially with
B^{2}
while

|R^{B}|_{t}\leq\exp(K_{T}B)\left\|G\right\|_{T,2}
, we deduce
that:

\sup_{t\leq T}|R|_{t}^{2}\leq K_{T,\epsilon}\|G\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{2}.

We can also deduce for fixed
c_{i-1}
,
c_{i}
,
R^{B}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

converges, as
B\to\infty
, to
R(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})
, and
R

satisfies Eq. (
6
).

Step 4: uniqueness.

Next, we show uniqueness of
R
. Assume that
R,R^{\prime}
are two solutions
to the equation in
L^{2}
, we have
U=R-R^{\prime}
satisfies

\partial_{t}U_{i}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=-\Delta F_{i}^{\left(1\right)}(U)(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

We then have

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}U_{i}(G,t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq K|U|_{t}|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}+K|U|_{t}|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+K|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}+K|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}+K|U_{i-1}(c_{i-1})|_{t}|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}.

Let
\kappa=\max_{t\leq T}(|R|_{t},|R^{\prime}|_{t})<\infty
. Then

\partial_{t}|U_{i}(c_{i-1})|_{t}^{2}\leq K|U_{i}(c_{i-1})|_{t}\left(\kappa+|U_{i-1}(c_{i-1})|_{t}+\mathbb{E}_{C}[|U_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}]^{1/2}\right),

\partial_{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}^{2}\leq K|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}\left(\kappa(1+\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}))^{2}+|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}+|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}\right).

In particular

\partial_{t}|U_{L}(c_{L})|_{t}^{2}\leq K|U_{L}(c_{L})|_{t}\left(\kappa(1+|w_{L}(c_{L})|_{t})+|U_{L}(c_{L})|_{t}\right),

from which we obtain
|U_{L}(c_{L})|_{t}\leq K_{\kappa,T}(1+\sup_{s\leq T}|w_{L}(c_{L})|_{s})

for all
t\leq T
and
c_{L}
. Note that
\mathbb{E}_{C_{L}}[K_{\kappa,T}(1+\sup_{s\leq T}|w_{L}(C_{L})|_{s})^{2}]<\infty
.
Assume the bound on
|U_{i+1}(c_{i+1})|_{t}
such that
\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[|U_{i+1}(C_{i+1})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+2}(C_{i+1})|_{t}^{2}\right]<\infty
,
we have

\partial_{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}^{2}\leq K|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}\left(\kappa(1+\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}))^{2}+|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}+|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}\right),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}\left(\kappa+|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}+\mathbb{E}[|U_{i+1}(C_{i+1})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+2}(C_{i+1})|_{t}^{2}]^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq K|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}\left(\kappa+|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}+K_{\kappa,T}\right).

Thus,
|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}\leq K_{\kappa,T}(\int_{0}^{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{s}ds+1)
.
Hence,

\partial_{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}\leq\kappa\left(1+\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}))^{2}+|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}+\int_{0}^{t}|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{s}ds\right).

From this we obtain

|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}\leq K_{\kappa,T}\exp(K_{\kappa,T}|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t})(1+\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}))^{2}).

Note that we still have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[|U_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{\kappa,T}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\exp(K_{\kappa,T}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t})(1+\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}))^{2})^{4}\right]

\displaystyle<\infty,

by Lemma
11
. By induction, we obtain bounds

\max_{t\leq T}\max(|U_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|U_{i}(c_{i})|_{t})\leq\exp\left(K_{\kappa,T}(1+\max(|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}))\right),

for all
i
and
c_{i}
. From here we can easily obtain a similar
bound on
U_{i}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})
,

\max_{t\leq T}|U_{i}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i})|\leq\exp\left(K_{\kappa,T}(1+\max(|w_{i}(c_{i-1})|_{t},|w_{i-1}(c_{i-1})|_{t},|w_{i+1}(c_{i})|_{t},|w_{i}(c_{i})|_{t}))\right).

This also implies all moments of
U
are finite again by Lemma
11
.

Using those bounds, we have, for any
B>0
, that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}|U|_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K|U|_{t}^{2}+K|U|_{t}\sum_{i}\left(\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[|U_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K(1+B)|U|_{t}^{2}+K|U|_{t}\sum_{i}\left(\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[|U_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}\mathbb{I}(|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}\geq B)\right]\right)^{1/2}.

\displaystyle\leq K(1+B)|U|_{t}^{2}+K\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[|U_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}\mathbb{I}(|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}\geq B)\right].

Our bound on
|U_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}
together with Lemma
11

gives

\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[|U_{i}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}^{2}\mathbb{I}(|w_{i+1}(C_{i})|_{t}\geq B)\right]\leq\exp(K_{\kappa,T}B-cB^{2}).

This implies

\partial_{t}|U|_{t}^{2}\leq K(1+B)|U|_{t}^{2}+\exp(K_{\kappa,T}B-cB^{2}).

Thus,

\sup_{t\leq T}|U|_{t}^{2}\leq\exp(K(1+B)T)\exp(K_{\kappa,T}B-cB^{2}).

Sending
B
to infinity, we immediately obtain that
|U|_{t}=0

for all
t\leq T
. Thus, the solution
R
is unique.
∎
Repeating the proof of Theorem
21
identically gives
the following.

Lemma 22
.

If
\|G\|_{T,2p+\epsilon}<\infty
, then
R_{i}(G,t,\cdot,\cdot)\in L^{2p}(\Omega_{i-1}\times\Omega_{i})
,
and

\|R(G)\|_{T,2p}^{2p}\leq K_{T,\epsilon}\|G\|_{T,2p+\epsilon}^{2p}.

Appendix D
Connecting finite-width neural network fluctuations with the limit
system: Proof of Theorem
5

Let us recall that

{\bf R}_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})=\sqrt{N}({\bf w}_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})-w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})).

Our goal is to prove Theorem
5
. A major technical
difficulty here lies in establishing suitable a priori moment bounds
for
\mathbf{R}
that are independent of
N
. This task suffers
from similar unboundedness issues that are encountered in the proof
of Theorem
21
. Again this is a problem unique to the
multilayer structure. Yet it requires a delicate argument that is
different from the one in the proof of Theorem
21
.
In particular, Lemma
23
below plays a crucial
role and computes very high moments (of order increasing with
N
)
of the neural network’s weights under GD evolution and randomized
initialization. It is also a result of independent interest, which
we expect to have applications beyond the current pursuit.

In the following, we denote

\|{\bf R}_{i}(t)-R_{i}(t)\|_{2p}^{2p}=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right|^{2p}\right].

For brevity, let us write

\tilde{w}_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})=w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})).

Similar to the development in Appendix
B
, define

\displaystyle\|\tilde{w}_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[\left|w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))\right|^{2p}\right],

\displaystyle\|{\bf w}_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},j_{i})\right|^{2p}\right],

\displaystyle\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(t,j_{i})\|_{2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{J_{i+1}}\left[\left|w_{i}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))\right|^{2p}\right],

\displaystyle\|{\bf w}_{i+1}(t,j_{i})\|_{2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{J_{i+1}}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,j_{i},J_{i+1})\right|^{2p}\right],

\displaystyle\|\tilde{w}_{i}(t)\|_{2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\|\tilde{w}_{i}(t,J_{i})\|_{2p}^{2p}\right],

\displaystyle\|{\bf w}_{i}(t)\|_{2p}^{2p}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\|\mathbf{w}_{i}(t,J_{i})\|_{2p}^{2p}\right].

We define similarly
\|{\bf R}_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2p}
,
\|R_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2p}
,

\|{\bf R}_{i+1}(t,j_{i+1})\|_{2p}
,
\|R_{i+1}(t,j_{i+1})\|_{2p}
,

\|{\bf R}_{i}(t)\|_{2p}
,
\|R_{i}(t)\|_{2p}
(where quantities
involving
R
should be computed w.r.t.
R(\tilde{G},t)
). We also
let

\|{\bf R}(t)\|_{2p}^{2p}=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\|{\bf R}_{i}(t)\|_{2p}^{2p},\qquad\|R(t)\|_{2p}^{2p}=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\|R_{i}(t)\|_{2p}^{2p}.

In all of these, when we drop the subscript
2p
, we implicitly take

2p=2
. For
B>0
, define

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbb{B}}_{i}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}(\|\tilde{w}_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k},\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k}\leq B),

\displaystyle{\bf B}_{i}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}(\|{\bf w}_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k},\|{\bf w}_{i+1}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k}\leq B),

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i})

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathbb{B}}_{i}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i}){\bf B}_{i}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{B}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i-1})\mathbb{B}^{B,2k}(t,j_{i}).

We drop the superscripts
B
and
2k
if they are clear from context.
Note that these are random variables due to the randomness of sampling

\{C_{i}(j_{i}):\;j_{i}\in[N_{i}],\;i\in[L]\}
, whose expectation
is denoted by
\mathbf{E}
.

D.1
High moments of neural network’s weights under GD

We obtain the following estimate, which is important for our development.

Lemma 23
.

For
k\geq 1
and
B>0
, we have:

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right|^{k}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{k}\right],

and for all
N\geq(kL)^{6}
,

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|^{k}\right]\leq k^{k/2}K_{T}^{kL}.

As an immediate corollary, for a fixed constant
C
and
B\leq N^{K_{C}}
,

{\bf E}\left[{\bf B}_{i}^{B,2C}(t,j_{i})\right]\leq\exp(-K_{T,C}B^{2}).

Proof.

The first equality is trivial. For the second bound, we consider
i\geq 2
;
the case
i=1
can be done identically. We have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=k\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})^{k-1}\partial_{t}{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq k\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|^{k}\right]^{(k-1)/k}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|\partial_{t}{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|^{k}\right]^{1/k}.

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|\partial_{t}{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,{\bf y})\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(J_{i-1},J_{i})}\right]\right|^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(J_{i-1},J_{i})}\right|^{2}\right]^{k/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i+1}(t,J_{i},J_{i+1})\right|{}^{k}\right]\prod_{j\geq i+2}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j}(t,J_{j-1},J_{j})^{2}\right]^{k/2}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|{}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq Kk\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|^{k}\right]^{(k-1)/k}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf w}_{i+1}(t,J_{i},J_{i+1})\right|^{k}\right]^{1/k}\prod_{j\geq i+2}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j}(t,J_{j-1},J_{j})^{2}\right]^{1/2}.

In particular,

\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{J}[\left|{\bf w}_{L}(t,J_{L-1},J_{L})\right|^{k}]^{1/k}\leq K_{T},

which implies

\mathbb{E}_{J}[\left|{\bf w}_{L}(t,J_{L-1},J_{L})\right|^{k}]^{1/k}\leq\mathbb{E}_{J}[\left|{\bf w}_{L}(0,J_{L-1},J_{L})\right|^{k}]^{1/k}+Kt.

Then inductively, we obtain that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}_{J}[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|{}^{k}]^{1/k}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{J}[\left|{\bf w}_{i}(0,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right|{}^{k}]^{1/k}+p_{i}\Big(\left(\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j}(0,J_{j-1},J_{j})^{2}\right]^{1/2}\right)_{j\geq i+1},\left(\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[|{\bf w}_{j}(0,J_{j-1},J_{j})|^{k}\right]^{1/k}\right)_{j\geq i+1},T\Big),

where
p_{i}
is a polynomial of degree at most
L-i
, and furthermore,
in each monomial in
p_{i}
there is at most one term of degree one
from the variables
\left(\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[|{\bf w}_{j}(0,J_{j-1},J_{j})|^{k}\right]^{1/k}\right)_{j\geq i+1}
.
This allows us to bound

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}[|{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{k}]

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}[|{\bf w}_{i}(0,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{k}]

\displaystyle\quad+K_{T}^{k}\sum_{j\geq i+1}{\bf E}\Big[\mathbb{E}_{J}[|{\bf w}_{i}(0,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{k}]\sum_{j^{\prime}\geq i+1}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[|{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,J_{j^{\prime}-1},J_{j^{\prime}})|^{2}\right]^{kL/2}\Big].

We have

\displaystyle{\bf E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J}[|{\bf w}_{i}(0,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{k}]\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[|{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,J_{j^{\prime}-1},J_{j^{\prime}})|^{2}\right]^{kL/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left({\bf E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J}[|{\bf w}_{i}(0,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{k}]^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left({\bf E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,J_{j^{\prime}-1},J_{j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]^{kL}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left({\bf E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J}[{\bf w}_{i}(0,J_{i-1},J_{i})^{2k}]\right]\right)^{1/2}\left({\bf E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,J_{j^{\prime}-1},J_{j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]^{kL}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}k^{k/2}\left({\bf E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,J_{j^{\prime}-1},J_{j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]^{kL}\right]\right)^{1/2}.

Let us analyze the term in the last display:

\mathbb{E}_{J^{\prime}}\left[{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,J_{j^{\prime}-1},J_{j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]^{kL}=N^{-2kL}\sum_{\alpha_{1},\dots,\alpha_{kL}\in[N_{j^{\prime}-1}],\beta_{1},\dots,\beta_{kL}\in[N_{j^{\prime}}]}\prod_{t=1}^{kL}{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,\alpha_{t},\beta_{t})^{2}.

Furthermore, if each index appears at most once among
\alpha,\beta
,
then

\displaystyle{\bf E}\left[\prod_{t=1}^{kL}{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,\alpha_{t},\beta_{t})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\prod_{t=1}^{kL}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[w_{j^{\prime}}(0,C_{j^{\prime}-1},C_{j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]^{kL}

\displaystyle\leq K^{kL}.

On the other hand, if some index appears at least twice, let
d(a)

be the number of times
a
appears among
\{\alpha_{s}\}
, and we
similarly define
d(b)
. We have by Finner’s inequality
2
2

2

Helmut Finner, "A generalization of Holder’s inequality
and some probability inequalities", The Annals of probability
(1992), pp. 1893–1901.
that

\displaystyle{\bf E}\left[\prod_{t=1}^{kL}{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,\alpha_{t},\beta_{t})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{t=1}^{kL}{\bf E}\left[{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,\alpha_{t},\beta_{t})^{2\max(d(\alpha_{t}),d(\beta_{t}))}\right]^{1/\max(d(\alpha_{t}),d(\beta_{t}))}

\displaystyle\leq K^{k(1+L)}\prod_{t=1}^{kL}\max(d(\alpha_{t}),d(\beta_{t})).

Note that
\sum_{a}d(a)=\sum_{b}d(b)=kL
. Fixing the multisets
\{d(a)\}=\{d(a):\;a\in[N_{j^{\prime}-1}]\},\;\{d(b)\}=\{d(b):\;b\in[N_{j^{\prime}}]\}
,
let
M_{a}
be the number of positive values in
\{d(a)\}
and
M_{b}

the number of positive values of
\{d(b)\}
. Let
H_{a}
be the
number of values at least
2
in
\{d(a)\}
and similarly for
H_{b}
.
The number of unlabeled bipartite graphs for which the degrees on
one part are given by
\{d(a)\}
and the degrees of the other part
are given by
\{d(b)\}
is at most
(H_{b}+1)^{H_{a}}(H_{a}+1)^{H_{b}}

(each half-edge from a vertex of degree at least
2
on the
a
-side
is chosen to match to a half-edge of a vertex of degree at least
2

on the
b
-side or a vertex of degree
1
, for a total of
H_{b}+1

choices). After choosing the unlabeled bipartite graph, there are
at most
N^{M_{a}+M_{b}}
ways to label the vertices of the graph
with indices in
[N_{j^{\prime}-1}]
or
[N_{j^{\prime}}]
. Let
Q
be the number
of edges between vertices both having degree
1
. We have at most

(kL)!/Q!
ways to assign index
t\in[kL]
to edges of the labeled
bipartite graph. We have that
Q\geq kL-\sum_{a}d(a)\mathbb{I}(d(a)\geq 2)-\sum_{b}d(b)\mathbb{I}(d(b)\geq 2)
,
while

M_{a}=\sum_{a}\mathbb{I}(d(a)\geq 1)\leq\sum_{a}\mathbb{I}(d(a)=1)+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{a}d(a)\mathbb{I}(d(a)\geq 2)=kL-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{a}d(a)\mathbb{I}(d(a)\geq 2),

and similarly
M_{b}\leq kL-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{b}d(b)\mathbb{I}(d(b)\geq 2)
,
so
M_{a}+M_{b}\leq 2kL-\frac{1}{2}\left(\sum_{a}d(a)\mathbb{I}(d(a)\geq 2)+\sum_{b}d(b)\mathbb{I}(d(b)\geq 2)\right)
.
Thus, the number of edges between vertices both having degree
1

is at least
2(M_{a}+M_{b})-3kL
. We also have
H_{a}\leq\frac{1}{2}\sum_{a}d(a)\mathbb{I}(d(a)\geq 2)\leq kL-M_{a}

and
H_{b}\leq kL-M_{b}
. Finally, note that

\prod_{t=1}^{kL}\max(d(\alpha_{t}),d(\beta_{t}))\leq\left(\frac{kL}{\min(1,H_{a})}\right)^{H_{a}}\left(\frac{kL}{\min(1,H_{b})}\right)^{H_{b}}.

Thus, using convention that
x!=1
if
x\leq 0
and using that the
number of choices of
\{d(a)\}
given
M_{a}=m_{a}
is at most
\binom{kL-1}{m_{a}-1}
,
we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\prod_{t=1}^{kL}\max(d(\alpha_{t}),d(\beta_{t}))

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\Gamma}\frac{(kL)!}{(2m_{a}+2m_{b}-3kL)!}N^{m_{a}+m_{b}}(h_{b}+1)^{h_{a}}(h_{a}+1)^{h_{b}}\left(\frac{kL}{h_{a}}\right)^{h_{a}}\left(\frac{kL}{h_{b}}\right)^{h_{b}}\binom{kL-1}{m_{a}-1}\binom{kL-1}{m_{b}-1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\Gamma}\frac{(kL)!}{(2m_{a}+2m_{b}-3kL)!}N^{m_{a}+m_{b}}\left(\frac{2(kL)^{2}}{\min(1,h_{a})}\right)^{h_{a}}\left(\frac{2(kL)^{2}}{\min(1,h_{b})}\right)^{h_{b}}\binom{kL-1}{m_{a}-1}\binom{kL-1}{m_{b}-1}

\displaystyle\leq K^{kL}\sum_{u=0}^{2kL}N^{2kL-u}\left(\frac{4(kL)^{4}}{\min(1,u)}\right)^{u}

\displaystyle\leq K^{kL}N^{2kL}\sum_{u=0}^{2kL}\left(\frac{4(kL)^{4}}{N\min(1,u)}\right)^{u},

where we use the shorthands
\Gamma=\left\{m_{a},m_{b}\in[kL],h_{a},h_{b}\leq kL/2,h_{a}\leq kL-m_{a},h_{b}\leq kL-m_{b}\right\}
.
Hence, for
N>(kL)^{6}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\prod_{t=1}^{kL}\max(d(\alpha_{t}),d(\beta_{t}))

\displaystyle\leq K^{kL}N^{2kL}.

In particular,

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j^{\prime}}(0,J_{j^{\prime}-1},J_{j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]^{kL}\leq K^{kL}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}[{\bf w}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})^{k}]

\displaystyle\leq k^{k/2}K_{T}^{kL}.

∎

Remark 24
.

The same argument shows that for
any
\ell\geq 1
and
N\geq(K_{\ell}kL)^{16}
,

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf w}_{j}(0,J_{j-1},J_{j})^{2\ell}\right]^{kL}\leq K_{\ell}^{kL}.

D.2
A priori estimates at the fluctuation level

Lemma 25
.

We have for any
t\leq T
:

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}(\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)-H_{i}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{T,k},

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{T,k},

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{T,k},

{\bf E}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\tilde{y}(t,x))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{T,k},

Proof.

Let us drop
t
and
x
from the notations for brevity. We also
recall Lemma
11
. We prove claim by claim:

•

We have:

\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}(\tilde{H}_{1}(C_{1}(J_{1}))-H_{1}(C_{1}(J_{1}))\right)^{2k}\right]=0

and using induction,

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}(\tilde{H}_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))-H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\bigg[\bigg(\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)\varphi_{i-1}\left(\tilde{H}_{i-1}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right)\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}\left(C_{i-1},C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)\varphi_{i-1}\left(H_{i-1}\left(C_{i-1}\right)\right)\right]\bigg)^{2k}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\left|w_{i}\left(C_{i-1},C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)\varphi_{i-1}\left(H_{i-1}\left(t,C_{i-1}\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)\left(\varphi_{i-1}\left(\tilde{H}_{i-1}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right)\right)\right)-\varphi_{i-1}\left(H_{i-1}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right)\right)\right)\right)\right]\right)^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,k}+K_{k}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[\left|w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)\right|^{2k}\right]\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[N\left(\tilde{H}_{i-1}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right)\right)-H_{i-1}\left(C_{i-1}\left(J_{i-1}\right)\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,k}.

•

Next we have:

\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}}{\partial\tilde{H}_{L}(1)}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i}(1)}\right)\right)^{2k}\leq K_{k}\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\tilde{H}_{L}(1)-H_{i}(1)\right)\right)^{2k}\leq K_{T,k}.

Using backward induction:

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i+1}(C_{i+1})}w_{i+1}\left(C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right),C_{i+1}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J_{i+1}}\left[\sqrt{N}\left|\frac{\partial\tilde{y}}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i+1}(C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i+1}(C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))}\right|\left|w_{i+1}\left(C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right),C_{i+1}\left(J_{i+1}\right)\right)\right|\right]^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{J_{i+1}}\left[\sqrt{N}\left|\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i+1}(C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))}\right|\left|w_{i+1}\left(C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right),C_{i+1}\left(J_{i+1}\right)\right)\right|\left|\tilde{H}_{i}\left(C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)-H_{i}\left(C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)\right|\right]^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,k}+K_{k}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i+1}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left|\frac{\partial\tilde{y}}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i+1}(C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i+1}(C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))}\right|\right)^{4k}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left|\tilde{H}_{i}\left(C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)-H_{i}\left(C_{i}\left(J_{i}\right)\right)\right|\right)^{8k}\right]^{1/3}

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,k}.

•

For the third claim:

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right)\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(c_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}\right)\right)^{2k}+K_{k}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial H_{i}(c_{i})}\right)^{2k}\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\tilde{H}_{i-1}\left(c_{i-1}\right)-H_{i-1}\left(c_{i-1}\right)\right)\right)^{2k}.

Combining the previous claims, it is then easy to see that

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\tilde{y}}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{T,k}.

•

The fourth claim is also immediate from the first claim by observing
that

\sqrt{N}\left|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(\cdot,\tilde{y})-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(\cdot,\hat{y})\right|\leq K\left|\sqrt{N}\left(\tilde{y}-\hat{y}\right)\right|\leq K\left|\sqrt{N}\left(\tilde{H}_{L}\left(1\right)-H_{L}\left(1\right)\right)\right|.

∎

Lemma 26

(Lipschitz bounds)
.

We have for any
t\leq T
:

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{i}(t,j_{i},x)-\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),x)\right)^{2k}\leq\|{\bf R}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}+K_{k}\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k})\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}\|_{2k}^{2k}),

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf H}_{i}(j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad\leq K_{k}\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\bigg(\|R\|_{2k}^{2k}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k})(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}\|_{4k}^{2k})\prod_{j\leq i-1}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2k}^{2k})\prod_{j\geq i+2}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{4k}^{4k})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{j\geq i+1}\mathbb{E}_{J_{j}}[\|R_{j}(J_{j})\|_{2}^{2k}\|\tilde{w}_{j+1}(J_{j})\|_{2k}^{2k}]\prod_{i+2\leq j^{\prime},j^{\prime}\neq j+1}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2k}^{2k})+\|R_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2}^{2k}\prod_{i+2\leq j^{\prime}}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2k}^{2k})\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\|{\bf R}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2}^{2k}\prod_{j\geq i+2}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2k}^{2k}),

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad\leq K_{k}\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf H}_{i}(j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial\tilde{H}_{i}(C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+K_{k}\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2}^{2k}\prod_{j\geq i+2}\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2}^{2k}\cdot\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{i-1}(t,j_{i-1},x)-\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),x)\right)^{2k},

\displaystyle\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\tilde{y}(t,x))\right)\leq K_{k}\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}\prod_{j=1}^{L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}\|_{2k}^{2k}).

In the above, we have dropped the notational dependency on
t
on
the right-hand side for brevity.

Proof.

Let us consider the first claim:

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{i}(t,j_{i},x)-\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),x)\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle=\left(\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}[{\bf w}_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))\varphi_{i-1}({\bf H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),x))-w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))\varphi_{i-1}(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),x))]\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf w}_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))-w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})))\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad+K^{k}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),x)-\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),x))\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))^{2}\right]\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf w}_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))-w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})))\right)^{2}\right]^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad+K^{k}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),x)-\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),x))\right)^{2}\right]^{k}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))^{2}\right]^{k}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{1}(t,j_{1},x)-\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{1}(j_{1}),x)\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}\left(\sqrt{N}(\varphi_{1}({\bf w}_{1}(t,C_{1}(j_{1}))\cdot x)-\varphi_{1}(w_{1}(t,C_{1}(j_{1}))\cdot x)\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}\left(\sqrt{N}\left|{\bf w}(t,C_{1}(j_{1}))-w_{1}(t,C_{1}(j_{1}))\right|\cdot|x|\right)^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K^{k}\left(\sqrt{N}\left|{\bf w}(t,C_{1}(j_{1}))-w_{1}(t,C_{1}(j_{1}))\right|\right)^{2k}.

Thus, by induction, we obtain that

\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{i}(t,j_{i},x)-\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),x)\right)^{2k}\leq\|{\bf R}_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}+K_{k}\|{\bf R}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k})\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}\|_{2k}^{2k}).

The remaining claims can be obtained similarly.
∎

Lemma 27

(A-priori moment estimate of
\mathbf{R}
)
.

We have for any
t\leq T
,

{\bf E}\|{\bf R}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}\leq K_{k,T}.

Proof.

In the following, let us drop the dependency on
t
for brevity.
From the bounds in Lemmas
26
and
25
,
we can obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}{\bf R}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle=\left|\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{\mathbf{y}}\left(X\right)\right)\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}\left(X\right)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}\left(j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)}-\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(X\right)\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}\right]\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\left|\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{\mathbf{y}}\left(X\right)\right)\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}\left(X\right)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}\left(j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)}-\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\tilde{y}\left(X\right)\right)\frac{\partial\tilde{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}\right]\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\left|\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\tilde{y}\left(X\right)\right)\frac{\partial\tilde{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}-\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(X\right)\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}\right]\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\left|\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}\left(X\right)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}\left(j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}\right]\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\left|\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{\mathbf{y}}\left(X\right)\right)-\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\tilde{y}\left(X\right)\right)\right)\frac{\partial\tilde{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}\right]\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\left|\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\tilde{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}\right]\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\left|\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\tilde{y}\left(X\right)\right)-\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(X\right)\right)\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}\left(X\right)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}\left(j_{i-1}\right),C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)}\right]\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k})\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}\prod_{j\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{4k}^{4k})

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\|{\bf R}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\prod_{j\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2k}^{2k})+K_{k}\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\sum_{j\geq i+1}\mathbb{E}_{J_{j}}[\|{\bf R}_{j}(J_{j})\|_{2}^{2k}\|\tilde{w}_{j+1}(J_{j})\|_{2k}^{2k}]\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2k}^{2k})

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\|{\bf R}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2}^{2k}\prod_{j\geq i+2}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2k}^{2k})+K_{k}\|{\bf R}_{i-1}(t,j_{i-1})\|_{2}^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i-1}(j_{i-1})\|_{2}^{2k})\prod_{j=1}^{L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2}^{2k})+A_{k}^{2k}\left(t,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right),

where
A_{k}\left(t,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)
does not depend on
\mathbf{R}

and satisfies the moment bounds in Lemma
25
,
i.e.
\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[A_{k}^{2k}\left(t,J_{i-1},J_{i}\right)\right]\leq K_{T,k}
.

For each
k
, consider the ODE, initialized at zero:

\partial_{t}X_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})=K_{k}F[X](t,j_{i-1},j_{i})+A_{k}\left(t,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right),

where

\displaystyle F[X](t,j_{i-1},j_{i})

\displaystyle=\bigg(\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k})\|X\|_{2k}^{2k}\prod_{j\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{4k}^{4k})\bigg)^{1/2k}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\|X_{i}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\prod_{j\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2k}^{2k})\bigg)^{1/2k}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\sum_{j\geq i+1}\mathbb{E}_{J_{j}}[\|X_{j}(J_{j})\|_{2k}^{2k}\|\tilde{w}_{j+1}(J_{j})\|_{2k}^{2k}]\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2k}^{2k})\bigg)^{1/2k}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\|X_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\prod_{j\geq i+2}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2k}^{2k})\bigg)^{1/2k}+K_{k}\|X_{i-1}(t,j_{i-1})\|_{2k}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}\|X\|_{2k}^{2k}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i-1}(j_{i-1})\|_{2}^{2k})\prod_{j=1}^{L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j}\|_{2}^{2k})\bigg)^{1/2k}.

The key property of
X
is that for all
t,j_{i-1},j_{i}
, we have:

|{\bf R}_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})|\leq|X_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})|.

For
B>0
, define

\partial_{t}X_{i}^{B}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})=K_{k}F[X^{B}](t,j_{i-1},j_{i})\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})+A_{k}\left(t,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right).

We have the estimate:

\partial_{t}\|X^{B}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}\leq K_{k}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})(1+B^{2k})\|X^{B}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}+\|A_{k}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}.

Here, we denote
\|\tilde{w}^{B}\|_{p}=\min(B,\|\tilde{w}\|_{p})
.
Furthermore, for solutions
X^{B},\;X^{\prime B}
to the equation, by the
triangle inequality, we have

\left|\partial_{t}\left(X^{B}-X^{\prime B}\right)(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})\right|\leq K_{k}F[\left(X^{B}-X^{\prime B}\right)](t,j_{i-1},j_{i})\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i}).

Existence and uniqueness of
X^{B}
follows easily from the above
Lipschitz estimate, and we furthermore have the bound

\|X^{B}(t)\|_{2k}\leq K_{k}\exp(K_{k}B\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\sup_{s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})^{1/2k}t)\cdot\|A_{k}(t)\|_{2k},

and similar to the proof of Theorem
21
, the bounds
(for sufficiently large
B
)

\|X_{i}^{B}(t,j_{i-1})\|_{2k}\leq K(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}(t,j_{i-1})\|_{2k}^{2k})(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i-1}(t,j_{i-1})\|_{2k}^{2k})\exp\Big(K_{k}B\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\sup_{s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})^{1/2k}t\Big)\|A_{k}(t)\|_{2k},

\|X_{i}^{B}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k}\leq K(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k})(1+\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(t,j_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k})\exp\Big(K_{k}B\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\sup_{s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})^{1/2k}t\Big)\|A_{k}(t)\|_{2k}.

Notice that
X^{B}
is positive and monotonically increasing in
B
.
For
B^{\prime}>B
and
\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})=\mathbb{I}(\mathbb{B}^{B^{\prime}}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})=1,\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})=0)
,
we have for
\Delta X=X^{B^{\prime}}-X^{B}
:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}_{J}[|\Delta X(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{2k}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J}[|\Delta X(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{2k}\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})]\right)^{(2k-1)/(2k)}

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg[\bigg(\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})(1+B^{\prime 2k})\|X^{B^{\prime}}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}+\|A_{k}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}\bigg)\mathbb{E}_{J}[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{J}\bigg[\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})(1+B^{\prime 2k})\|X^{B^{\prime}}(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\Big(\|X_{i}^{B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}+\|X_{i+1}^{B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i})\|_{2k}^{2k}+\|X_{i-1}^{B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i-1})\|_{2k}^{2k}\Big)\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\bigg]\bigg]^{1/(2k)},

while

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathbb{E}[|\Delta X(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{2k}\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\bigg[\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})(1+B^{\prime 2k})\bigg]\mathbb{E}[|\Delta X(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})|^{2k}\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})],

and if
\mathbb{B}^{B^{\prime}}(t,\cdot)=0
then
\partial_{t}\Delta X(t,\cdot)=0
.
Thus,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\|\Delta X(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\bigg[\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}(t)\|_{4k}^{4k})(1+B^{\prime 2k})\bigg]^{1/2k}\|\Delta X(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}

\displaystyle\quad+K_{k}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})]\exp\Big(K_{T}B^{\prime}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\sup_{s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(s)\|_{4k}^{4k})^{1/2k}t\Big).

This gives:

\|\Delta X(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}\leq K_{k}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(s,J_{i-1},J_{i})]\exp(K_{T}B^{\prime}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\sup_{s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(s)\|_{4k}^{4k})^{1/2k})ds.

Therefore,

{\bf E}\|\Delta X(t)\|_{2k}^{2k}\leq K_{k,T}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(s,J_{i-1},J_{i})]ds\Big)^{1/2}{\bf E}\Big[\exp(K_{T}B^{\prime}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\sup_{s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(s)\|_{4k}^{4k})^{1/2k})\Big]^{1/2}.

Notice that

\exp\Big(K_{T}B^{\prime}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\sup_{s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(s)\|_{4k}^{4k})^{1/2k}\Big)\leq\exp\Big(K_{T}B^{\prime}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(0)\|_{Kk}^{Kk})^{1/2k}\Big).

Furthermore Lemma
23
implies that

\Big(\int_{0}^{t}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(s,J_{i-1},J_{i})]ds\Big)^{1/2}\leq\exp(-K_{T}B^{2}),

for all
B\leq N^{1/16}
, while
