{\bf E}\Big[\exp\Big(K_{T}B^{\prime}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(0)\|_{Kk}^{Kk})^{1/2k}\Big)\Big]\leq{\bf E}\Big[\exp\Big(K_{T}B^{\prime}(1+\sum_{j^{\prime}\leq L}\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(0)\|_{Kk}^{K})\Big)\Big].

Using Remark
24
, and
\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(0)\|_{Kk}^{K}\leq B^{\prime}{}^{K}
,
we have that if
N\geq(K_{T}B^{\prime C})^{16}
for some sufficiently large
constant
C>0
, then

{\bf E}\Big[\exp\Big(K_{T}B^{\prime}\prod_{j^{\prime}\leq L}(1+\|\tilde{w}_{j^{\prime}}^{B^{\prime}}(0)\|_{Kk}^{Kk})^{1/2k}\Big)\Big]\leq\exp(K_{T}B^{\prime}).

Combining the estimates, we obtain that for all
B\leq K_{T}N^{c}

for a sufficiently small constant
c>0
,

{\bf E}\|X^{B}\|_{2k}^{2k}\leq{\bf E}\|X^{K_{T}}\|_{2k}^{2k}+K_{k}\int_{K_{T}}^{B}\exp(K_{T}x)\exp(-Kx^{2})dx\leq K_{T}.

Finally, for
\gamma>0
consider the event
{\cal E}
that
\max_{i}\max_{j_{i}}\max(\|\tilde{w}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{Kk},\|\tilde{w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{Kk},\|{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i})\|_{Kk},\|{\bf w}_{i+1}(j_{i})\|_{Kk})\leq\gamma
.
This event has probability at least
1-KN\exp(-K_{k}\gamma)
for

\gamma\leq N^{c_{k}}
with sufficiently small
c_{k}
. We have for

B=K_{T}N^{c}
that

{\bf E}\left[\left|\|X\|_{2k}^{2k}-\|X^{B}\|_{2k}^{2k}\right|\mathbb{I}({\cal E})\right]\leq\exp(-KB^{2})\exp(K_{T}\gamma^{K}).

For
\gamma=N^{\epsilon}
with
\epsilon
sufficiently small compared
to
c
and
c_{k}
, we then have

{\bf E}\left[\left|\|X\|_{2k}^{2k}-\|X^{B}\|_{2k}^{2k}\right|\mathbb{I}({\cal E})\right]\leq 1.

This immediately implies

{\bf E}\left[\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}\mathbb{I}({\cal E})\right]\leq{\bf E}\left[\|X\|_{2k}^{2k}\mathbb{I}({\cal E})\right]\leq K_{T}.

On the other hand,

{\bf E}\left[\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}(1-\mathbb{I}({\cal E}))\right]\leq{\bf E}[\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{4k}]^{1/2}(1-{\bf P}({\cal E}))^{1/2}.

Recall the definition of
{\bf R}
, and noting that the moments of

{\bf w}
and
w
are finite by Lemma
23
,
we have the trivial bound

{\bf E}[\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{4k}]^{1/2}\leq N^{K_{k}}K_{T}.

Thus

{\bf E}\left[\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}(1-\mathbb{I}({\cal E}))\right]\leq N^{K_{k}}K_{T}\exp(-c_{k}N^{2\epsilon})<1.

We can thus conclude that

{\bf E}[\|{\bf R}\|_{2k}^{2k}]<K_{k,T}.

∎

D.3
Comparison bounds and concentration estimates

Let us recall the notation
\mathbf{O}_{T}(\cdot)
from Appendix

B
.

Lemma 28
.

We have for any
t\leq T
,

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}({\bf H}_{i}(t,J_{i},x)-H_{i}(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k,T},

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf H}_{i}(J_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial H_{i}(C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k,T},

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(J_{i-1},J_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k,T},

{\bf E}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))\right)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k,T}.

Proof.

This is a consequence of Lemmas
25
,

26
,
27
and
23
.
∎

Lemma 29
.

We have for
t\leq T
,

\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))\right)=\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)-\hat{y}(t,x))\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))+{\bf O}_{T}(1/\sqrt{N}).

Proof.

We have

\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))\right)=\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)-\hat{y}(t,x))\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(y,y^{\prime}(t,x)),

for some
y^{\prime}(t,x)
between
\hat{y}(t,x)
and
\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)
.
Hence,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right)-\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)-\hat{y}(t,X))\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[N(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)-\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\cdot\left(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)-\hat{y}(t,X)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{N}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)-\hat{y}(t,X))\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K_{T}}{N},

where the last step follows easily from Lemma
28
.
∎

Lemma 30
.

We have for
t\leq T
,

\sqrt{N}\left(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)-\tilde{y}(t,x)\right)=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]+{\bf O}_{T}(1/\sqrt{N}).

Proof.

Following the same argument as Lemma
29
, we
get:

\displaystyle\sqrt{N}\left(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)-\tilde{y}(t,x)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]+{\bf O}_{T}(1/\sqrt{N}).

Again the same argument applied to each of the summands leads to:

\displaystyle{\bf E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\left({\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left({\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}/N,

by Lemmas
27
and
28
.
∎

Lemma 31
.

We have for
t\leq T
,

\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}).

Proof.

We verify the condition of Lemma
12

for
{\bf i}=(i-1,i)
,
{\bf i}^{\prime}=\emptyset
,
S=\{C_{k}(j_{k}):\;j_{k}\in[N_{k}],\;k\in[L]\}

and

f(S,c_{i-1},c_{i})=R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}.

We have from Lemma
22
and Theorem
18
:

{\bf E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{4}\right]\right]\leq K_{T},

and therefore, with Lemma
11
,

{\bf E}\left[\left|f(S^{j_{{\bf i}}},C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))\right|^{2}\right]\leq K_{T}.

Let
\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}}
be obtained similar to
\tilde{G}

but with
C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}})
replaced by an independent copy

C_{{\bf i}}^{\prime}(j_{{\bf i}})
. It remains to bound

{\bf E}\left[\left|f(S,C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))-f(S^{j_{{\bf i}}},C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))\right|^{2}\right]={\bf E}\left[\left|R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))}-R_{i}(\tilde{G}^{j_{\mathbf{i}}},t,C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))}\right|^{2}\right],

which – similar to the previous second moment bound – amounts
to proving

{\bf E}\left[\left|R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))-R_{i}(\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))\right|^{4}\right]\leq K_{T}/N^{2}.

The proof is complete upon having this bound.

By linearity from Theorem
18
,

R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})=R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,c_{i-1},c_{i}),

and we also have:

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left((\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}})^{y}(t,X)+\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{j}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left((\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}})_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X)+\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{j}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}(c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-2}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}(C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right].

Note that

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{j}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{j}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}}

\displaystyle=0,\qquad\forall j\leq i-1,

\displaystyle(\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\bigg(w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}\left(\tilde{H}_{i-1}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),x)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}^{\prime}(j_{i-1}),c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}\left(\tilde{H}_{i-1}^{j_{{\bf i}}}(t,C_{i-1}^{\prime}(j_{i-1}),x)\right)\bigg),

\displaystyle(\tilde{G}_{i+1}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i+1}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,c_{i+1},x)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left(w_{i+1}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),c_{i+1})\varphi_{i}\left(\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),x)\right)-w_{i}(t,C_{i}^{\prime}(j_{i}),c_{i+1})\varphi_{i}\left(\tilde{H}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}}}(t,C_{i}^{\prime}(j_{i}),x)\right)\right),

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{j_{i}^{\prime}\neq j_{i}}w_{i+1}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}^{\prime}),c_{i+1})\left(\varphi_{i}\left(\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}^{\prime}),x)\right)-\varphi_{i}\left(\tilde{H}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}}}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}^{\prime}),x)\right)\right),

\displaystyle(\tilde{G}_{\ell+1}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{\ell+1}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,c_{\ell+1},x)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{j_{\ell}}w_{\ell+1}(t,C_{\ell}(j_{\ell}),c_{\ell+1})\left(\varphi_{\ell}\left(\tilde{H}_{\ell}(t,C_{\ell}(j_{\ell}),x)\right)-\varphi_{\ell}\left(\tilde{H}_{\ell}^{{\bf i}}(t,C_{\ell}(j_{\ell}),x)\right)\right),\qquad\forall\ell\geq i+1.

We bound their expected
2p
-powers. We have that for
\ell\geq i+1
,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{\ell+1}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{\ell+1}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{\ell+1}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{\ell+1}(J_{\ell+1}),x)\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,p}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{\ell}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{\ell}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{\ell}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{\ell}(J_{\ell}),x)\right|^{2p+2}\right],

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{\ell+1}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{\ell+1}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{\ell+1}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{\ell+1},x)\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,p}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{\ell}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{\ell}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{\ell}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{\ell}(J_{\ell}),x)\right|^{2p+2}\right].

For
\ell=i
, by Lemma
11
,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i+1}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{i+1}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i+1}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}),x)\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,p}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right|^{2p+2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+K_{T,p}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i+1}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left(w_{i+1}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))\varphi_{i}\left(\tilde{H}_{i}(t,C_{i}(j_{i}),x)\right)-w_{i}(t,C_{i}^{\prime}(j_{i}),C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}))\varphi_{i}\left(\tilde{H}_{i}^{j_{\mathbf{i}}}(t,C_{i}^{\prime}(j_{i}),x)\right)\right)\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,p}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right|^{2p+2}\right]+K_{T,p}N^{-p}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{i+1}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i+1}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{i+1},x)\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq KK_{T,p}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right|^{2p+2}\right]+K_{T,p}N^{-p}.

Finally at
\ell=i-1
, by the same argument:

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{i}(J_{i}),x)\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,p}N^{-p},

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\left|(\tilde{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}_{i}^{j_{{\bf i}},\tilde{H}})(t,C_{i}(C_{i}),x)\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T,p}N^{-p}.

As such, all these expected
2p
-powers are bounded by
K_{T,p}N^{-p}
.
We derive similar bounds for
\tilde{G}^{\partial\tilde{H}}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}},\partial\tilde{H}}
,

\tilde{G}^{w}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}},w}
.

The estimates in Theorem
21
imply that

\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{\left|R_{j}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{\mathbf{i}}},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\right|^{2}\right\}\leq\inf_{B}\left\{\exp(K_{T}B)\|\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{\mathbf{i}}}\|_{T,2}^{2}+\exp(-K_{T}B^{2})\|\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{\mathbf{i}}}\|_{T,4}^{2}\right\}\leq K_{T}N^{-1}.

Similarly, letting

\displaystyle M_{1}(c_{i})

\displaystyle=\sup_{t\leq T}\max(\|w_{i}(t,c_{i})\|_{2},\|w_{i+1}(t,c_{i})\|_{2},|w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),c_{i})|,|w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}^{\prime}),c_{i})|),

\displaystyle M_{2}(c_{i-1})

\displaystyle=\sup_{t\leq T}\max(\|w_{i}(t,c_{i-1})\|_{2},\|w_{i-1}(t,c_{i-1})\|_{2},|w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(j_{i}))|,|w_{i}(t,c_{i-1},C_{i}(j_{i}^{\prime}))|),

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-1}\exp(K_{T}M_{1}(c_{i})),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i+1}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-1}\exp(K_{T}M_{2}(c_{i})),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-2}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i-1}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-1}\exp(K_{T}M_{1}(c_{i-1})).

All of these estimates give a final bound:

\partial_{t}\left|R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\right|\leq N^{-1/2}\left[K_{T}+\exp(K_{T}M_{1}(c_{i}))+\exp(K_{T}M_{2}(c_{i}))+\exp(K_{T}M_{1}(c_{i-1}))\right].

In particular, since it is initialized at zero, we have:

\left|R_{i}(\tilde{G}-\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})\right|^{4}\leq N^{-2}\left[K_{T}+\exp(K_{T}M_{1}(c_{i}))+\exp(K_{T}M_{2}(c_{i}))+\exp(K_{T}M_{1}(c_{i-1}))\right].

Lemma
11
implies that
\mathbf{E}\left[\exp(K_{T}M_{1}(C_{i}(j_{i}))\right]\leq K_{T}

and similarly for other terms. We thus get:

{\bf E}\left[\left|R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))-R_{i}(\tilde{G}^{j_{{\bf i}}},t,C_{{\bf i}}(j_{{\bf i}}))\right|^{4}\right]\leq K_{T}/N^{2},

as desired.
∎

Lemma 32
.

We have for
t\leq T
and any
B>1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]

\displaystyle=K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2}+\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}).

Proof.

We have:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right|^{2}\right].

For
i=L
:

\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right|^{2}\right]\leq K

and so the conclusion follows from Lemma
31
.

For
i<L
, we have for any
B>1
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}_{J}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|w_{i+1}(C_{i}(J_{i}),C_{i+1})\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq KB+K\mathbb{E}_{J}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|w_{i+1}(C_{i}(J_{i}),C_{i+1})\right|^{2}\right]\mathbb{I}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|w_{i+1}(C_{i}(J_{i}),C_{i+1})\right|^{2}\right]\geq B\right).

Therefore, by Lemmas
11
,
27

and Theorem
21
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq KB\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+K_{T}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbb{I}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|w_{i+1}(C_{i}(J_{i}),C_{i+1})\right|^{2}\right]\geq B^{2}\right)\right]^{1/4}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})\right)^{4}+\left(R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{4}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq KB\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]+e^{-K_{T}BN},

for a suitable finite constant
B=B\left(T\right)
. The conclusion
again follows from Lemma
31
.
∎

Lemma 33
.

We have for
t\leq T
:

\displaystyle\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial w(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[\sum_{r=1}^{L}R_{r}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,x\right)}{\partial w_{r}\left(C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i-1}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-2},C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,x\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}\left(C_{i-2},C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i}(j_{i})\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,x\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}\left(C_{i-1},C_{i}(j_{i})\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i+1}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{i}(j_{i}),C_{i+1}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,x\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}\left(C_{i}(j_{i}),C_{i+1}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}).

Proof.

By Lemma
28
, we have:

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\bigg[\bigg(\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}(J_{i-1},J_{i})}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial w(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{J^{\prime}}\bigg[{\bf R}_{j}(t,J_{j-1}^{\prime},J_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}(J_{j-1}^{\prime}),C_{j}(J_{j}^{\prime}))\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{J^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathbf{R}_{i-1}\left(t,J_{i-2}^{\prime},J_{i-1}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,x\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}\left(C_{i-2}(J_{i-2}^{\prime}),C_{i-1}(J_{i-1})\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{J^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathbf{R}_{i}\left(t,J_{i-1}^{\prime},J_{i}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,x\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}^{\prime}),C_{i}(J_{i})\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{J^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathbf{R}_{i+1}\left(t,J_{i},J_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,x\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}\left(C_{i}(J_{i}),C_{i+1}(J_{i+1}^{\prime})\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i})\right)}\bigg]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}N^{-1}.

Following the argument in Lemmas
31
and
32
,
we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{J^{\prime}}\bigg[{\bf R}_{j}(t,J_{j-1}^{\prime},J_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}(J_{j-1}^{\prime}),C_{j}(J_{j}^{\prime}))\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{C^{\prime}}\bigg[R_{j}(\tilde{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2}+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}),

and similarly for the rest of the terms.
∎

D.4
Proof of Theorem
5

Proof of Theorem
5
.

Recall that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\bf R}_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})-\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))\right)

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right)\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\tilde{G}_{i}^{w}\left(t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}),X\right)\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right)\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\right)\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right)\right].

Comparing with Eq. (
6
):

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\bf R}_{i}(t,j_{i-1},j_{i})-\partial_{t}R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[A_{1}\left(Z,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)+A_{2}\left(Z,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)+A_{3}\left(Z,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)\right],

in which

\displaystyle A_{1}\left(z,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,x))\bigg\{\sqrt{N}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\tilde{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[\sum_{r=1}^{L}R_{r}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial w_{r}\left(C_{r-1},C_{r}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i-1}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-2},C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}\left(C_{i-2},C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i}(j_{i})\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}\left(C_{i-1},C_{i}(j_{i})\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[R_{i+1}\left(\tilde{G},t,C_{i}(j_{i}),C_{i+1}\right)\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}\left(t,X\right)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}\left(C_{i}(j_{i}),C_{i+1}\right)\partial w_{i}\left(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i})\right)}\bigg]\bigg\},

\displaystyle A_{2}\left(z,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}A_{2,1}\left(z\right),

\displaystyle A_{2,1}\left(z\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))\right)-\left(\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+\tilde{G}^{y}\left(t,x\right)\right)\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))

\displaystyle A_{3}\left(z,j_{i-1},j_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\left(\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x))-\partial_{2}{\cal L}(y,\hat{y}(t,x))\right)\left(\frac{\partial\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)}{\partial{\bf w}_{i}(j_{i-1},j_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}(j_{i-1}),C_{i}(j_{i}))}\right).

To analyze
A_{2}
, we have from Lemmas
29
,

30
and
32
:

A_{2,1}\left(z\right)=K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2}+{\bf O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}).

Furthermore, for any
B>1
, by Lemma
11
, Theorems

18
and
21
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z,J}\left[\left|A_{2}\left(Z,J_{i-1},J_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z,J}\left[\left|A_{2}\left(Z\right)\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|w_{i+1}(C_{i}(J_{i}),C_{i+1})\right|^{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}B^{2}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|A_{2,1}\left(Z\right)\right|^{2}\right]+K_{T}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|A_{2,1}\left(Z\right)\right|^{4}\right]^{1/2}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbb{I}\left(\mathbb{E}_{J_{i}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}}\left[\left|w_{i+1}(C_{i}(J_{i}),C_{i+1})\right|^{2}\right]\geq B^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}B^{2}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|A_{2,1}\left(Z\right)\right|^{2}\right]+e^{-K_{T}BN}.

Hence with a suitable choice of
B
,

\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z,J}\left[\left|A_{2}\left(Z,J_{i-1},J_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\right]\leq K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]+K_{T}N^{-1}.

The treatment of
A_{1}
is similar via Lemma
33
:

\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z,J}\left[\left|A_{1}\left(Z,J_{i-1},J_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\right]\leq K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]+K_{T}N^{-1}.

Finally for
A_{3}
, by Lemmas
28
,

A_{3}=\boldsymbol{O}_{T}(N^{-1/2}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i})\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left|\partial_{t}{\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-\partial_{t}R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i})\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{T}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i}))\right)^{2}\right]+K_{T}N^{-1},

By Gronwall’s inequality, we have

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{J}\left[\left({\bf R}_{i}(t,J_{i-1},J_{i})-R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1}(J_{i-1}),C_{i}(J_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\frac{K_{T}}{N}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix E
CLT for the output fluctuation: Proof of Theorem
6

E.1
Joint convergence in moment: Proof of Proposition
8

Proof of Proposition
8
.

In the following, let
\tilde{R}
denote
R(\tilde{G},\cdot)
and
– with an abuse of notations –
R
denote
R(G,\cdot)
. By Theorem

21
— and recalling the norms defined in its statement
— there exists a sequence in
B
of processes
\tilde{R}^{B}

with
\tilde{R}^{B}(t)=\mathfrak{L}_{t}^{B}(\tilde{G})
where
\mathfrak{L}_{t}

is a linear map for all
t\leq T
with
\|\tilde{R}^{B}\|_{T,2}\leq\exp(K_{T}B)\|\tilde{G}\|_{T,2}

and
\|\tilde{R}^{B}-\tilde{R}\|_{T,2}\leq\|\tilde{G}\|_{T,2+\epsilon}\exp(-c\epsilon B^{2})

for some
c>0
. We have:

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},\tilde{R}_{i(j)}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},\tilde{R}_{i(j)}^{B}+(\tilde{R}_{i(j^{\prime})}^{B}-\tilde{R}_{i(j^{\prime})})\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\left(\langle h_{j^{\prime}},\tilde{R}_{i(j)}^{B}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}+\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}\|\tilde{G}\|_{T,2+\epsilon}\exp(-c\epsilon B^{2})O_{j^{\prime}}\left(1\right)\right)^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},\tilde{R}_{i(j)}^{B}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]+O_{D}(1)\exp(-c\epsilon B^{2})\prod_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\|\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\|\tilde{G}\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle(\mathfrak{L}_{t}^{B})^{*}(h_{j^{\prime}}),\tilde{G}_{i(j^{\prime})}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]+O_{D}(1)\exp(-c\epsilon B^{2})\prod_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\|\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\|\tilde{G}\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}},

where
(\mathfrak{L}_{t}^{B})^{*}
is the adjoint of
\mathfrak{L}_{t}^{B}
,
which has operator norm at most
\exp(K_{T}B)
. A similar bound applies
to
\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},G_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},R_{i(j^{\prime})}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]
.
Since
\tilde{G}
converges
G
-polynomially in moment to
G
,
we have:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},\tilde{R}_{i(j)}^{B}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},G_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},R_{i(j)}^{B}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq N^{-1/8}O_{D}(\max_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{D},\max_{j^{\prime}}\|(\mathfrak{L}_{t}^{B})^{*}(h_{j^{\prime}})\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{D})

\displaystyle\leq\exp(K_{T}B)N^{-1/8}O_{D}(\max_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{D},\max_{j^{\prime}}\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{D}).

This also shows

\|\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\leq\|G_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}+N^{-1/8}O_{D}(\max_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{D},\max_{j^{\prime}}\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{D}).

Then choosing
B=c_{0}\sqrt{\log N}
for a suitable constant
c_{0}
,
for sufficiently large
N
, we have:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},\tilde{G}_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},\tilde{R}_{i(j)}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\prod_{j}\langle f_{j},G_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\rangle_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\langle h_{j^{\prime}},R_{i(j)}\rangle_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\exp(K_{T}B)N^{-1/8}O_{D}(\max_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{D},\max_{j^{\prime}}\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{D})+O_{D}(1)\exp(-c\epsilon B^{2})\prod_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\|G_{i(j)}^{\alpha_{j}}\|_{t_{j}}^{\beta_{j}}\prod_{j^{\prime}}\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}\|G\|_{T,2+\epsilon}^{\beta_{j^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-1/8+o(1)}O_{D}(\max_{j}\|f_{j}\|_{t_{j}}^{D},\max_{j^{\prime}}\|h_{j^{\prime}}\|_{t_{j^{\prime}}}^{D}).

In particular,
(R(\tilde{G},\cdot),\tilde{G})
converges
G
-polynomial-moment
and
R
-linear-moment to
(R(G,\cdot),G)
.
∎

E.2
CLT for the output function: Proof of Theorem
6

Proof of Theorem
6
.

By Lemma
30
, Lemma
32
and Theorem

5
, we have:

\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)-\tilde{y}(t,x))=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+{\bf O}_{T}(1/\sqrt{N}).

This implies that

\underbrace{\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)-\hat{y}(t,x))}_{\equiv U(z)}=\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\tilde{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+\tilde{G}^{y}(t,x)}_{\equiv V(\tilde{G},z)}+{\bf O}_{T}(1/\sqrt{N}).

For
U=U(Z)
and
V=V(\tilde{G},Z)
, we have for
m\geq 1
and
1
-bounded

h
,

\displaystyle\left|{\bf E}\left[\langle h,U^{m}\rangle_{Z}-\langle h,V^{m}\rangle_{Z}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|U^{m}-V^{m}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{m}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\left|U\right|^{m-1}+\left|V\right|^{m-1}\right)\left|U-V\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{m}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|U\right|^{2m-2}+\left|V\right|^{2m-2}\right]^{1/2}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left|U-V\right|^{2}\right]^{1/2}.

Recalling Lemmas
11
,
28
,
Theorems
21
and
18
, we obtain:

\left|{\bf E}\left[\langle h,U^{m}\rangle_{Z}-\langle h,V^{m}\rangle_{Z}\right]\right|\leq K_{T,m}N^{-1/2}.

Finally using Proposition
8
and Theorem

18
, we obtain:

\left|{\bf E}\left[\langle h,U^{m}\rangle_{Z}-\langle h,V(\underline{G},Z)^{m}\rangle_{Z}\right]\right|\leq K_{T,m}N^{-1/8+o(1)}.

This also proves the desired weak convergence.
∎

Appendix F
Asymptotic variance of the output fluctuation: Proof of Theorems

9
and
10

F.1
Variance decomposition of the limiting output fluctuation
\underline{G}^{y}

The goal of this section is to show that
\underline{G}^{y}\left(t,\cdot\right)

lies in the span of
\frac{\partial\hat{y}\left(t,\cdot\right)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}
.
This is done by decomposing the covariance structure of
\underline{G}^{y}

in a suitable layer-wise fashion.

Proposition 34
.

Let
f
be any square-integrable function
of
X
for which

\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}f(X)\right]^{2}\right]=0.

Then almost surely,

\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)f(X)\right]^{2}=0.

Proof.

Let us drop the notational dependency on
t
. Let us also write

\varphi_{i}\left(H_{i}(x,c_{i})\right)\equiv\varphi_{i}\left(x,c_{i}\right),\qquad\varphi_{i}^{\prime}\left(H_{i}(x,c_{i})\right)\equiv\varphi_{i}^{\prime}\left(x,c_{i}\right).

Note that

\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\underline{G}^{y}(X)f(X)\right]^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{X,X^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{E}[\underline{G}^{y}(X)\underline{G}^{y}(X^{\prime})]f(X)f(X^{\prime})\right].

We recall from the statement of Theorem
18
:

\displaystyle\underline{G}^{y}(x)

\displaystyle=\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x)\underline{G}_{L}^{\tilde{H}}(x),

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{i}^{\tilde{H}}(c_{i},x)

\displaystyle=\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(c_{i},x)+\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}^{\prime}(x,C_{i-1})\underline{G}_{i-1}^{\tilde{H}}(x,C_{i-1})\right],

\displaystyle\underline{G}_{1}^{\tilde{H}}(c_{1},x)

\displaystyle=0,

which thus yield

\underline{G}^{y}(x)=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\varphi^{\prime}_{L}(x)\mathbb{E}_{C}\bigg[\bigg(\prod_{j=i+1}^{L}w_{j}(C_{j-1},C_{j})\varphi^{\prime}_{j-1}(x,C_{j-1})\bigg)\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(x,C_{i})\bigg],

where we take
\prod_{j=L+1}^{L}=1
. We also recall:

\mathbf{E}[\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(x,c_{i})\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(x^{\prime},c_{i}^{\prime})]=\mathbb{C}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},c_{i})\varphi_{i-1}\left(x,C_{i-1}\right);\;w_{i}(C_{i-1},c_{i}^{\prime})\varphi_{i-1}\left(x^{\prime},C_{i-1}\right)\right],

and for
i\neq j
,

\mathbf{E}[\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(x,c_{i})\underline{G}_{j}^{H}(x^{\prime},c_{j}^{\prime})]=0.

Combining these facts together, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}[\underline{G}^{y}(x)\underline{G}^{y}(x^{\prime})]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x)\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x^{\prime})\mathbb{E}_{C,C^{\prime}}\bigg[\prod_{j=i+1}^{L}w_{j}(C_{j-1},C_{j})\varphi^{\prime}_{j-1}(x,C_{j-1})w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\varphi_{j-1}^{\prime}(x,C_{j-1}^{\prime})\mathbb{E}\left[\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(x,C_{i})\underline{G}_{i}^{H}(x,C_{i}^{\prime})\right]\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x)\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x^{\prime})\mathbb{E}_{C_{i},\dots,C_{L},C_{i}^{\prime},....,C_{L}^{\prime}}\bigg[\prod_{j=i+1}^{L}w_{j}(C_{j-1},C_{j})\varphi^{\prime}_{j-1}(x,C_{j-1})w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\varphi_{j-1}^{\prime}(x,C_{j-1}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i}^{\prime})\varphi_{i-1}(x,C_{i-1})\varphi_{i-1}(x^{\prime},C_{i-1})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(x,C_{i-1})\right]\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i}^{\prime})\varphi_{i-1}(x^{\prime},C_{i-1})\right]\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\bigg[\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x)\mathbb{E}_{C_{i},\dots,C_{L}}\bigg[\prod_{j=i+1}^{L}w_{j}(C_{j-1},C_{j})\varphi^{\prime}_{j-1}(x,C_{j-1})\cdot w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(x,C_{i-1})\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x^{\prime})\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}^{\prime},\dots,C_{L}^{\prime}}\bigg[\prod_{j=i+1}^{L}w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\varphi^{\prime}_{j-1}(x^{\prime},C_{j-1}^{\prime})\cdot w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i}^{\prime})\varphi_{i-1}(x^{\prime},C_{i-1})\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\bigg[\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x)\mathbb{E}_{C_{i},\dots,C_{L}}\bigg[\prod_{j=i+1}^{L}w_{j}(C_{j-1},C_{j})\varphi^{\prime}_{j-1}(x,C_{j-1})\cdot w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\varphi_{i-1}(x,C_{i-1})\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}^{\prime}}\bigg[\varphi_{L}^{\prime}(x^{\prime})\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}^{\prime},\dots,C_{L}^{\prime}}\bigg[\prod_{j=i+1}^{L}w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\varphi^{\prime}_{j-1}(x^{\prime},C_{j-1}^{\prime})\cdot w_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},C_{i}^{\prime})\varphi_{i-1}(x^{\prime},C_{i-1}^{\prime})\bigg]\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}^{\prime}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(x^{\prime})}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i}^{\prime})}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]\right]\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}^{\prime}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}^{\prime}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},C_{i}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(x^{\prime})}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},C_{i}^{\prime})}\right]\right]\bigg\}.

In particular,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X,X^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{E}[\underline{G}^{y}(X)\underline{G}^{y}(X^{\prime})]f(X)f(X^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X,C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}f(X)\right]^{2}\right]-\left(\mathbb{E}_{X,C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}f(X)\right]\right)^{2}\right\}.

Of course
\mathbb{E}_{X,X^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{E}[\underline{G}^{y}(X)\underline{G}^{y}(X^{\prime})]f(X)f(X^{\prime})\right]\geq 0
,
but we also have:

\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X,C_{i}}\left[w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}f(X)\right]^{2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\left|w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}f(X)\right]^{2}\right]\right]=0.

Therefore it must be that

\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\underline{G}^{y}(X)f(X)\right]^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{X,X^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{E}[\underline{G}^{y}(X)\underline{G}^{y}(X^{\prime})]f(X)f(X^{\prime})\right]=0.

∎

F.2
Variance reduction under GD: Proof of Theorem
9

We prove the variance reduction effect of GD in the case of idealized
initialization.

Proof of Theorem
9
.

By Theorem
6
, the fluctuation
\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,x)-\hat{y}(t,x))

converges in polynomial-moment to

\hat{G}(t,x):=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+\underline{G}^{y}(t,x),

where
\underline{G}
is the limiting Gaussian process defined in
Theorem
18
. Furthermore, by Theorem
19
,

\partial_{t}\hat{G}(t,x)=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\partial_{t}R_{i}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}+R_{i}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\partial_{t}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)\right]+\partial_{t}\underline{G}^{y}(t,x).

By the assumption on the initialization,
\partial_{t}\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)=0

and
\partial_{t}\underline{G}^{y}=0
. Then using Eq. (
6
)
for
\partial_{t}R_{i}(\underline{G},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})
and recalling
that
\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(0,X\right)\right)\middle|X\right]=0
,
we obtain:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{G}(t,x)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C}\bigg[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigg(\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)+\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\bigg[R_{j}(\underline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\bigg]\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\hat{G}(t,X)\right],

where we denote
\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}=\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\left(Y,\hat{y}\left(0,X\right)\right)\middle|X\right]>0

a finite constant. Hence,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}V^{*}\left(t\right)

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{X}\big[\big|\hat{G}(t,X)\big|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle=-2\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z^{\prime}}\left[\hat{G}(t,X^{\prime})\left(\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X^{\prime})}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\hat{G}(t,X)\right]\right)\right]

\displaystyle=-2\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\hat{G}(t,X)\right]^{2}\right],

which is non-negative. This completes the proof of the first part.

Next, since we initialize the MF limit at a stationary point, we have

\hat{y}(t,x)=\hat{y}(x),\qquad\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,x)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}=\frac{\partial\hat{y}(x)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})},

independent of time
t
. Let
\mathfrak{A}:L^{2}({\cal P})\to L^{2}({\cal P})

be the linear operator defined by

(\mathfrak{A}f)(x)=-\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}f(X)\right].

As shown – and assuming
\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}=1
for simplicity
– we can write:

\partial_{t}\hat{G}(t,x)=(\mathfrak{A}\hat{G}\left(t,\cdot\right))(x).

Note that
\mathfrak{A}
is self-adjoint:

\mathbb{E}\left[g(X)(\mathfrak{A}f)(X)\right]=-\mathbb{E}_{Z,Z^{\prime},C}\left[g(X)f(X^{\prime})\sum_{i}\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X^{\prime})}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathfrak{A}g)(X)f(X)\right],

and
\mathfrak{A}
is compact, as it is a finite sum of integral operators
with Hilbert-Schmidt kernels. By the spectral theorem, the orthogonal
complement of
\ker(\mathfrak{A})
has a countable orthonormal basis
consisting of eigenfunctions of
\mathfrak{A}
. Note that
\ker(\mathfrak{A})

is a closed subspace of
L^{2}({\cal P})
which is separable, and
hence it is also separable. Similarly, the orthogonal complement of

\ker(\mathfrak{A})
is a separable subspace of
L^{2}({\cal P})
. For
any eigenfunction
\phi
of
\mathfrak{A}
with eigenvalue
\lambda
,
we have

(\mathfrak{A}\phi)(x)=-\sum_{i}\frac{\partial\hat{y}(x)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\phi(X)\right]=\lambda\phi(x).

Thus,

\lambda=\mathbb{E}[\phi(x)(\mathfrak{A}\phi)(x)]=-\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\phi(X)\right]^{2}\right].

Hence, all eigenvalues of
\mathfrak{A}
are non-positive. Note that

\mathfrak{A}f=0
if and only if

\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}f(X)\right]^{2}\right]=0.

By Proposition
34
, almost surely,
\underline{G}^{y}\in\ker(\mathfrak{A})^{\perp}

(noting that
\ker(\mathfrak{A})
is separable). Hence so is
\hat{G}(t,\cdot)
.
For
f\in\ker(\mathfrak{A})^{\perp}
, the solution to the equation
\partial_{t}f_{t}(x)=(\mathfrak{A}f_{t})(x)

is given as

f_{t}(x)=\exp(t\mathfrak{A})f_{0}(x).

Let
f_{0}=\sum_{j}\tau_{j}\phi_{j}
where
\phi_{j}
are eigenfunctions
of
\mathfrak{A}
in
{\cal H}
with eigenvalue
\lambda_{j}<0
, we
have

f_{t}(x)=\sum_{j}\exp(t\lambda_{j})\tau_{j}\phi_{j}(x).

In particular, since
\sum_{j}\tau_{j}^{2}=\left\|f_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}({\cal P})}^{2}

is finite, as
t\to\infty
,

\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\left|f_{t}(X)\right|^{2}\right]=\sum_{j}\exp(2t\lambda_{j})\tau_{j}^{2}\to 0.

This proves the second part of the theorem.
∎

F.3
Variance reduction under GD: Proof of Theorem
10

We prove the variance reduction effect of GD in the case of sufficiently
fast convergence to global optima. In particular, we recall from Theorem

10
that we assume for some
\eta>0
,

\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{2+\eta}\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}]ds<\infty.

(8)

The dependency on time will be important in this section, therefore
we remind that
K_{T}
denotes a constant depending on time, and

K
denotes a constant that does not.

Lemma 35
.

If
\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}]\right)^{1/2}dt<\infty
,
then for any
p\geq 1
,
\sup_{t\geq 0}\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}_{C}[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})^{2p}]\leq(Kp)^{p}
.

Proof.

We have

\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})=\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right],

and hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left|\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]^{p}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)^{2}\right]^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]^{p}\mathbb{E}_{C,Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]^{p}\cdot K\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1},C_{i}}\left[w_{i+1}(t,C_{i},C_{i+1})^{2p}\right]\prod_{j\geq i+2}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j},C_{j-1}}\left[w_{j}(t,C_{j-1},C_{j})^{2}\right]^{p}.

Let us consider
i>1
; the case
i=1
is similar. We then have:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})^{2p}]\right)

\displaystyle=2p\mathbb{E}[w_{i}^{2p-1}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})]

\displaystyle\leq 2pK\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})^{2p}]\right)^{(2p-1)/(2p)}\cdot(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})^{2p}])^{1/(2p)}

\displaystyle\leq 2pK\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})^{2p}]\right)^{(2p-1)/(2p)}\left(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1},C_{i}}\left[w_{i+1}(t,C_{i},C_{i+1})^{2p}\right]^{1/2p}\prod_{j\geq i+2}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j},C_{j-1}}\left[w_{j}(t,C_{j-1},C_{j})^{2}\right]^{1/2}.

In particular, for
i=L
,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{L}(t,C_{L-1},C_{L})^{2p}]\right)

\displaystyle=2pK\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{L}(t,C_{L-1},C_{L})^{2p}]\right)^{(2p-1)/(2p)}\left(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}]\right)^{1/2},

which implies that

\mathbb{E}[w_{L}(t,C_{L-1},C_{L})^{2p}]^{1/(2p)}\leq Kp^{1/2}+K\int_{0}^{t}\left(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}]\right)^{1/2}ds,

and therefore,

\sup_{t\geq 0}\mathbb{E}[w_{L}(t,C_{L-1},C_{L})^{2p}]^{1/(2p)}\leq Kp^{1/2}+K.

(Here we note that
\mathbb{E}[w_{L}(0,C_{L-1},C_{L})^{2p}]^{1/(2p)}\leq Kp^{1/2}

by the sub-Gaussian initialization assumption.)

Next, by induction, assuming that

\sup_{i>h}\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}[w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})^{2p}]^{1/(2p)}<Kp^{1/2}+K,

we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{h}(t,C_{h-1},C_{h})^{2p}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2pK\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{h}(t,C_{h-1},C_{h})^{2p}]\right)^{(2p-1)/(2p)}\left(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathbb{E}_{C_{h+1},C_{h}}\left[w_{h+1}(t,C_{h},C_{h+1})^{2p}\right]^{1/2p}\prod_{j\geq h+2}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j},C_{j-1}}\left[w_{j}(t,C_{j-1},C_{j})^{2p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2pK\left(\mathbb{E}[w_{h}(t,C_{h-1},C_{h})^{2p}]\right)^{(2p-1)/(2p)}\left(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}]\right)^{1/2}\mathbb{E}_{C_{h+1},C_{h}}\left[w_{h+1}(t,C_{h},C_{h+1})^{2p}\right]^{1/2p}.

Thus, as above,

\mathbb{E}[w_{h}(t,C_{h-1},C_{h})^{2p}]^{1/(2p)}\leq Kp^{1/2}+(Kp^{1/2}+K)\int_{0}^{t}\left(\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}]\right)^{1/2}ds,

so we get

\sup_{t\geq 0}\mathbb{E}[w_{h}(t,C_{h-1},C_{h})^{2p}]^{1/(2p)}\leq Kp^{1/2}+K.

This proves the claim.
∎

Under the assumption (
8
), the MF limit
w_{i}(t,\cdot,\cdot)

converges in
L^{2}
to a limit
\overline{w}_{i}
, as shown in
the corollary below.

Corollary 36
.

Under the setting of Lemma
35
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|w_{t}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})-\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{\delta}^{k}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]^{k}ds.

Proof.

We have from the previous lemma that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\partial_{t}w_{i}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2p}\right]^{1/(2p)}\leq K_{p}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]^{1/2}.

By Holder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\left|w_{t}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})-\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2k}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{-1-\delta}ds\right)^{2k-1}\left(\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}|\partial_{t}w_{i}(s,C_{i-1},C_{i})|^{2k}ds\right)

\displaystyle\leq K_{\delta}^{k}\left(\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}|\partial_{t}w_{i}(s,C_{i-1},C_{i})|^{2k}ds\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|w_{t}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})-\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{\delta}^{k}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[|\partial_{t}w_{i}(s,C_{i-1},C_{i})|^{2k}\right]ds

\displaystyle\leq K_{\delta}^{k}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]^{k}ds.

∎

This suggests that one should have convergence to a limit as
t\to\infty

at the fluctuation level. Should we have that, we would obtain Theorem

10
as a consequence of Theorem
9
.
The caveat is that the convergence must satisfy certain
uniform-in-time

properties. This is the bulk of the work.

Now we let
\overline{G},\overline{w},\overline{R}
be obtained by
plugging the infinite-time limit
\overline{w}=\left\{\bar{w}_{i}\right\}

into
w=\left\{w_{i}\left(t,\cdot,\cdot\right)\right\}
. Recall
that
\underline{G}
is the limiting Gaussian process defined in
Theorem
18
. We then have:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}R_{i}(\underline{G},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left(\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)+\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{j}(\underline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left(\underline{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X)+\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{j}(\underline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i}(\underline{G},t,c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}(c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-2}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{i}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}(C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right],

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\overline{R}_{i}(\overline{G},t,c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\cdot\left(\overline{G}^{y}(t,X)+\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{\overline{R}_{j}(\overline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\right],

where
\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}>0
denotes the positive constant
at global optima as in Assumption
2
. Whenever
the context is clear, we write
R
for
R(\underline{G},\cdot)
.
We also recall from Theorem
6
that the limiting output
fluctuation
\hat{G}
is described via
R(\underline{G},\cdot)
.

Let us rewrite these dynamics in the following form:

\partial_{t}R=\mathfrak{A}_{t}R+H_{t},\qquad\partial_{t}\overline{R}=\overline{\mathfrak{A}}\overline{R}+\overline{H},

which implies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(R-\overline{R})

\displaystyle=(\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})R+(H_{t}-\overline{H})+\overline{\mathfrak{A}}(R-\overline{R}),

\displaystyle(R-\overline{R})(s)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{\infty}\exp(-t\overline{\mathfrak{A}})((\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})R+(H_{t}-\overline{H}))dt,

for linear operators
\mathfrak{A}_{t}
and
\overline{\mathfrak{A}}
defined
by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{t}S(c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{i}(c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}(c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-2}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{i}(C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}(C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right],

\displaystyle\overline{\mathfrak{A}}S(c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\cdot\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{S(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\right],

\displaystyle H_{t}(c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X)\right],

\displaystyle\overline{H}(c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\cdot\overline{G}^{y}(X)\right].

Lemma 37
.

For any
\delta>0
and
k\geq 1
, we have the
estimate

\displaystyle{\bf E}\|H_{t}-\overline{H}\|_{2k}^{2k}

\displaystyle:={\bf E}\bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\cdot\overline{G}^{y}(X)\right]\right)^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X)\right]\right)^{2k}\right]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq K_{k,\delta}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]^{k}ds.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\cdot\overline{G}^{y}(X)\right]\right)^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\right)\cdot\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)\right]^{2k}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\cdot\left(\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)-\overline{G}^{y}(X)\right)\right]^{2k}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)^{2}\right]^{k}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\right)^{2}\right]^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)-\overline{G}^{y}(X)\right)^{2}\right].

Note that
\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)^{2}\right]^{k}<\infty

since
W(t)
is uniformly bounded in
L^{2p}
for any
p\geq 1
by
Lemma
35
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\right)^{2}\right]^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i},Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)^{2k}\right]+\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\left(\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\right)\right)^{2}\right]^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i},Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)^{2k}\right]+K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\right)^{2k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i},Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)^{2k}\right]+K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\hat{y}(t,X)-\overline{\hat{y}}(X)\right)^{2k}\right].

Using uniform boundedness of
W(t)
in
t
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i},Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k}\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1},C_{j}}\left[(w_{j}(t,C_{j-1},C_{j})-\overline{w}_{j}(C_{j-1},C_{j}))^{2k}\right],

and

\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\hat{y}(t,X)-\overline{\hat{y}}(X)\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k}\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1},C_{j}}\left[(w_{j}(t,C_{j-1},C_{j})-\overline{w}_{j}(C_{j-1},C_{j}))^{2k}\right].

By Lemma
36
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\frac{\partial\hat{y}(X)}{\partial\overline{w}_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\right)^{2}\right]^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K_{\delta}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]^{k}ds.

Next, we have

\displaystyle{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)-\overline{G}^{y}(X)\right)^{2}\right]^{k}

\displaystyle\leq K_{k,\delta}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[(\partial_{t}\underline{G}^{y}(s,X))^{2k}\right]ds.

Using the covariance structure of
\underline{G}^{\partial_{t}H}

and
\underline{G}^{\partial_{t}(\partial H)}
, we similarly deduce
that

{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[(\partial_{t}\underline{G}^{y}(t,X))^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]^{k}.

Furthermore,

\sum_{i=1}^{L}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X)\right]\right)^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]^{k},

by Lemma
35
. Hence,

\mathbf{E}\left[\|H_{t}-\overline{H}\|_{2k}^{2k}\right]\leq K_{k,\delta}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]^{k}ds.

∎

Note that under Assumption (
8
),
\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]\to 0

as
s\to\infty
, and hence

\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]^{k}ds\leq K_{k}+\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{(2k-1)(1+\delta)}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds.

Thus, under the assumption, we have that for
k=1+\epsilon
with

\epsilon
sufficiently small, and taking
\delta
sufficiently
small, we conclude that
{\bf E}\|H_{t}-\overline{H}\|_{2k}^{2k}\to 0

as
t\to\infty
for all
k=k(\eta)
sufficiently close to
1
.

Lemma 38
.

We have
\sup_{t}\|\overline{R}(t)\|_{2}^{2}/\|\overline{H}\|_{2}^{2}<\infty
.

Proof.

We have that
\overline{R}
is the solution to

\partial_{t}\overline{R}=\overline{\mathfrak{A}}\overline{R}+\overline{H}.

In the proof of Theorem
9
, we observe
that
\overline{\mathfrak{A}}
is self-adjoint and has nonpositive eigenvalues.
Hence,

\overline{R}/\|\overline{H}\|_{2}=\exp(t\overline{\mathfrak{A}})\overline{H}/\|\overline{H}\|_{2}

is bounded uniformly in time.
∎

Lemma 39
.

We have the following estimate for
\delta

sufficiently small (depending on
\eta
):

{\bf E}\|(\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})R\|_{2}^{2}\leq K_{\delta}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds+K_{\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right].

Proof.

We first follow the proof of Theorem
21
to give uniform-in-time
estimates for
R(t)
under the assumption (
8
).
For a threshold
B
, define the linear operator

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}_{t}^{B}S(c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{\mathfrak{A}}_{t}^{B}S(c_{i-1},c_{i}),

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathfrak{A}}_{t}^{B}S(c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\Bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i}(C_{i-1}^{\prime},c_{i})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i+1}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{i}(c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i+1}(c_{i},C_{i+1}^{\prime})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-2}^{\prime}}\left\{S_{i}(C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\frac{\partial^{2}\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial_{*}w_{i-1}(C_{i-2}^{\prime},c_{i-1})\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\right\}\right]\Bigg\}\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i}),

and

\displaystyle H_{t}(c_{i-1},c_{i})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(c_{i-1},c_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\cdot\underline{G}_{i}^{w}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i},X)\right],

\displaystyle\partial_{t}R^{B}(\underline{G},t,\cdot,\cdot)

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{A}_{t}^{B}(R^{B}(\underline{G},t))+H(\underline{G},t).

Following Theorem
21
, we have the bound

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\mathfrak{A}}_{t}^{B}(R^{B}(\underline{G},t))(c_{i-1},c_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq K\Big(\|R^{B}(t)\|\|w_{i}(t,c_{i})\|\|w_{i+1}(t,c_{i})\|+\|R_{i}^{B}(t,c_{i})\|\|w_{i+1}(t,c_{i})\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\|R_{i+1}^{B}(t,c_{i})\|+\|R_{i}^{B}(t,c_{i-1})\|\|w_{i}(t,c_{i})\|\Big)\cdot\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|\mathbb{B}^{B}(t,c_{i-1},c_{i}).

Thus

\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\left|\tilde{\mathfrak{A}}_{t}^{B}(R^{B}(\underline{G},t))(C_{i-1},C_{i})\right|^{2}\right]\leq K\|R^{B}(t)\|^{2}(1+B^{2})\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|^{2},

where we have used uniform-in-time boundedness of
W(t)
from Lemma

35
. We then have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\|R^{B}(t)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[R_{i}^{B}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,y)\cdot\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{j}^{B}(\underline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\|R^{B}(t)\|\cdot K\|R^{B}(t)\|(1+B)\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|+\|H(\underline{G},t)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{R_{j}^{B}(\underline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)^{2}+K\|w(t)\|_{K}^{K_{L}}\|\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\|\|R^{B}(t)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+K\|R^{B}(t)\|^{2}(1+B)\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|+\|H(\underline{G},t)\|^{2}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\|R^{B}(\underline{G},t)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K(1+B)\|R^{B}(t)\|^{2}\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|+K\|w(t)\|_{K}^{K_{L}}\|\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\|_{2}^{2}+\|H(\underline{G},t)\|^{2}.

Using that

\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\|dt<\infty,

{\bf E}\int_{0}^{\infty}\|H(\underline{G},t)\|^{2}dt<\infty,

\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|dt<\infty,

as well as Lemma
35
, we can then conclude

\sup_{t}{\bf E}\|R^{B}(\underline{G},t)\|_{2}^{2}\leq\exp(K(1+B)).

Next, we compare
R^{B}
and
R^{B^{\prime}}
as in Theorem
21

for
B^{\prime}>B
. We have the bound, recalling the definition of
\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}

in Theorem
21
,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\bf E}\|(R^{B}-R^{B^{\prime}})(t)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\sum_{i=1}^{L}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z,C_{i-1},C_{i}}\Bigg[(R_{i}^{B}-R_{i}^{B^{\prime}})(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\cdot\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,y)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{(R_{j}^{B}-R_{j}^{B^{\prime}})(\underline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+K{\bf E}\|R^{B}(t)\|^{2}(1+B)\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|

\displaystyle\quad+K_{\delta}\sum_{i=1}^{L}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i-1},C_{i}}\left[\mathbb{D}_{B,B^{\prime}}(t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\exp(K(1+B^{\prime}))\int_{0}^{t}(\|\underline{G}_{i+1}(s,C_{i})\|^{2}+\|\underline{G}_{i}(s,C_{i})\|^{2})ds\right]\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|

\displaystyle\leq-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}{\bf E}\left[\left(\sum_{j=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime}}\left\{(R_{j}^{B}-R_{j}^{B^{\prime}})(\underline{G},t,C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{j}^{\prime}(C_{j-1}^{\prime},C_{j}^{\prime})}\right\}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+K\|w(t)\|_{K}^{K_{L}}\|\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))-\partial_{2}^{2}{\cal L}\|_{2}{\bf E}\|R^{B}-R^{B^{\prime}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+K{\bf E}\|(R^{B}-R^{B^{\prime}})(t)\|^{2}(1+B)\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|+\exp(K(1+B^{\prime})-cB^{2})\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|.

Again using our convergence assumption, we can conclude that

\sup_{t}{\bf E}\|(R^{B}-R^{B^{\prime}})(t)\|^{2}\leq\exp(K(1+B))\exp(K(1+B^{\prime})-cB^{2}).

In particular,
R^{B}
converges (uniformly in time
t
) in
L^{2}

to the limit
R
, whose norm is uniformly bounded in time.

An identical argument shows that upon having finite moment
\|H_{t}\|^{2k+\delta}
,
we have that
R^{B}
converges (uniformly in time) in
L^{2k}
to
a limit
R
, whose
L^{2k}
norm is uniformly bounded in time. This
is guaranteed for all
k
sufficiently close to
1
(in terms of

\eta
) by the remark following Lemma
37
.

From Holder’s Inequality, we have the following estimate for sufficiently
small
\delta
:

\|\tilde{\mathfrak{A}}_{t}R(t)\|_{2}^{2}\leq K_{\delta}\|R(t)\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\|\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))\|^{2},

and

\|((\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\tilde{A}_{t})-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})R(t)\|_{2}^{2}\leq K_{\delta}\|R(t)\|_{2}^{2}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds.

Thus,

{\bf E}\|(\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})R(t)\|_{2}^{2}\leq K_{\delta}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds+\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right].

∎

Lemma 40
.

We have for
\delta
sufficiently small in
\eta
that

{\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}\leq K_{\delta}\left(\int_{t}^{\infty}(s^{2+\delta}-t^{2+\delta}+1)\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds\right).

Proof.

We have

\partial_{t}{\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}=2{\bf E}\langle R(t)-\overline{R},(\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})R(t)+(H_{t}-\overline{H})+\overline{\mathfrak{A}}(R(t)-\overline{R})\rangle.

We now bound

{\bf E}\langle R(t)-\overline{R},H_{t}-\overline{H}\rangle\leq K_{\delta}\left(\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds\right)^{1/2}\cdot({\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2})^{1/2},

We also have

\langle R(t)-\overline{R},\overline{\mathfrak{A}}(R(t)-\overline{R})\rangle\leq 0.

From Lemma
39
,

\displaystyle{\bf E}\left[\langle R(t)-\overline{R},(\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})\overline{R}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq K{\bf E}\left[\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}\right]^{1/2}{\bf E}\left[\|(\mathfrak{A}_{t}-\overline{\mathfrak{A}})\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K{\bf E}\left[\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}\right]^{1/2}\left(K_{\delta}\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds+K_{\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}.

Thus,

\partial_{t}{\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}\leq K_{\delta}\left(\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]+\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds\right)^{1/2}({\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2})^{1/2}.

In particular,

\partial_{t}({\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2})^{1/2}\leq K_{\delta}\left(\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(t,X))^{2}\right]+\int_{t}^{\infty}s^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds\right).

Hence,

{\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}\leq K_{\delta}\left(\int_{t}^{\infty}(s^{2+\delta}-t^{2+\delta}+1)\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds\right).

∎

We can now finish the proof of Theorem
10
.

Proof of Theorem
10
.

We have from Theorem
6
:

\lim_{N\to\infty}{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\left(\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)-\hat{y}(t,X))\right)^{2}\right]={\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)\right)^{2}\right].

For
\delta>0
sufficiently small in
\eta
,

\displaystyle\Bigg|{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[R_{i}(\underline{G},t,C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\hat{y}(t,X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+\underline{G}^{y}(t,X)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-{\bf E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{C}\left[\overline{R}_{i}(\overline{G},C_{i-1},C_{i})\frac{\partial\overline{\hat{y}}(X)}{\partial w_{i}(C_{i-1},C_{i})}\right]+\overline{G}^{y}(X)\right)^{2}\right]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq K{\bf E}\|R(t)-\overline{R}\|_{2}^{2}+K\|w(t)-\overline{w}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K_{\delta}\left(\int_{t}^{\infty}(s^{2+\delta}-t^{2+\delta}+1)\mathbb{E}_{Z}\left[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}\right]ds\right).

The conclusion immediately follows from the assumption

\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{2+\eta}\mathbb{E}[\partial_{2}{\cal L}(Y,\hat{y}(s,X))^{2}]ds<\infty,

together with Theorem
9
.
∎

Appendix G
Experimental details

We give a full description of the experimental setup in Section
5
.

Overall setup.

We consider a training set of 100 randomly chosen MNIST images of
digits 0, 4, 5 and 9, where we encode the label
y=+1
if the image
is digit 5 or 9 and
y=-1
otherwise. This small scale allows us
to run full-batch GD in reasonable time and avoid stochastic algorithms
which create extra fluctuations that are not the focus of our study,
as mentioned in Section
1
.

We use a neural network of 3 layers and
\tanh
activations in the
hidden layers (i.e.
\varphi_{1}=\varphi_{2}=\tanh
and
\varphi_{3}={\rm identity}
).
We include the bias in the first layer’s weight, and there is no bias
elsewhere. The network has constant widths
N
which we vary in the
plots of Fig.
1
. We train it with full-batch
(discrete-time) GD, a Huber loss
{\cal L}(y,y^{\prime})={\rm Huber}(y-y^{\prime})

and a constant learning rate
7\times 10^{-3}
. This learning rate
is sufficiently small so that we obtain smooth evolution plots in
Fig.
1
(a), suggesting a behavior close to the
continuous-time GD. We also define

{\rm Huber}(u)=\begin{cases}u^{2}/2,&|u|\leq 1,\\
|u|-1/2,&{\rm otherwise}.\end{cases}

All the training is run with one NVIDIA Tesla P100 GPU.

Initialization.

In Fig.
1
(a)-(c), we initialize the network
randomly as follows. We generate
\mathbf{w}_{1}(0,\cdot,\cdot)\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1/785)
,

\mathbf{w}_{2}(0,\cdot,\cdot)\sim\mathcal{N}(0.1,1)
and
\mathbf{w}_{3}(0,\cdot,\cdot)\sim\mathcal{N}(0.1,1)

all independently. In Fig.
1
(e), we first train
a big network which has width
N^{*}=5000
and has the same configuration
as described for Fig.
1
(a)-(c). We stop the
training of this width-
N^{*}
network at iteration
10^{5}
, which
ensures that it has found a global optimum. Then we consider a network
which has width
N\in\{50,100,200,400\}
and is initialized as follows:

•

Let
\mathbf{w}_{i}^{*}(T_{e},\cdot,\cdot)
be the
i
-th layer’s
weight of the width-
N^{*}
network at
T_{e}
corresponding to
iteration
10^{5}
.

•

For each
i=1,2
, we draw at random
\ell_{i,1},...,\ell_{i,N}

from
[N^{*}]
independently. Let
\ell_{0,\cdot}=\ell_{3,\cdot}=1
.

•

We set the initial weight
\mathbf{w}_{i}(0,j_{i-1},j_{i})=\mathbf{w}_{i}^{*}(T_{e},\ell_{i-1,j_{i-1}},\ell_{i,j_{i}})

for the width-
N
network.

In other words, the width-
N
network at initialization is a result
of subsampling the neurons of the width-
N^{*}
network at time
T_{e}
.
The width-
N^{*}
network is essentially an approximation of the
MF limit. Ideally when
N^{*}\to\infty
then
N\to\infty
, following

[
17
]
, we have that the width-
N
network is
initialized at a global optimum. Here this holds approximately at
finite widths, but is sufficient for our purpose.

Estimation for the plots.

For Fig.
1
(a), we run once for each
N
and
plot the training loss over time.

For Fig.
1
(b), we draw at random a single test
image
x_{{\rm test}}
(which is not among the 100 training images
and happens to be a digit 5 in this plot). For each
N
, we train
the network till time
T_{b}
, which corresponds to iteration
10^{3}
,
and we repeat
M=1000
times with
M
different randomization seeds.
Let
\hat{\mathbf{y}}_{N}^{k}(T_{b},x_{{\rm test}})
be the prediction
of
x_{{\rm test}}
of the
k
-th repeat, for
k\in[M]
. Let

\hat{\mathbf{r}}_{N}^{k}(T_{b},x_{{\rm test}})=\sqrt{N}\bigg(\hat{\mathbf{y}}_{N}^{k}(T_{b},x_{{\rm test}})-\frac{1}{M}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{M}\hat{\mathbf{y}}_{N}^{k^{\prime}}(T_{b},x_{{\rm test}})\bigg).

Then we plot the histogram of
\big\{\hat{\mathbf{r}}_{N}^{k}(T_{b},x_{{\rm test}})\big\}_{k\in[M]}
.

For Fig.
1
(c), we follow a procedure similar
to that of Fig.
1
(b), but with
M=10
. For
each
N
and each
x
from the training image set, we obtain
\hat{\mathbf{y}}_{N}^{k}(t,x)

for
k\in[M]
. Then we compute:

v_{N}(t)=\frac{1}{100M}\sum_{k=1}^{M}\sum_{x\in{\rm training\,set}}|\hat{\mathbf{r}}_{N}^{k}(t,x)|^{2}.

We take
v_{N}(t)
as an estimate for the output variance
\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}\big[\big|\sqrt{N}(\hat{{\bf y}}(t,X)-\hat{y}(t,X))\big|^{2}\big]
.

For Fig.
1
(e), we follow the same procedure
as Fig.
1
(c), except that we train a single
width-
N^{*}
network that is used for all
M
repeats and all
N\in\{50,100,200,400\}
.

For Fig.
1
(d), we recall the initialization
procedure for Fig.
1
(e). Here instead of using
one value of the terminal time
T_{e}
, we shall let it vary. Following
this procedure, for each
T_{e}
and each
N
, we obtain an untrained
width-
N
network. For each
T_{e}
, we repeat the procedure for

M=10
times, but we fix the same width-
N^{*}
network (terminated
at time
T_{e}
) for all
M
repeats. Let
\tilde{\mathbf{y}}_{N}^{k}(T_{e},x)

be the prediction of the untrained width-
N
network at the training
image
x
, in the
k
-th repeat with the terminal time
T_{e}
.
Using these predictions, one computes:

\displaystyle\tilde{v}_{N}(T_{e})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{100M}\sum_{k=1}^{M}\sum_{x\in{\rm training\,set}}|\tilde{\mathbf{r}}_{N}^{k}(T_{e},x)|^{2},

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{r}}_{N}^{k}(T_{e},x)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}\bigg(\tilde{\mathbf{y}}_{N}^{k}(T_{e},x)-\frac{1}{M}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}}_{N}^{k^{\prime}}(T_{e},x)\bigg).

We take
\tilde{v}_{N}(T_{e})
as an estimate for
\mathbf{E}\mathbb{E}_{Z}[|\tilde{G}^{y}\left(T_{e},X\right)|^{2}]
.

Reproducibility.

The experiments can be repeated using the codes in this link:
https://github.com/npminh12/nn-clt
